Idempotency Identity / Schema Version / Retention Owner / Security Classification / Related DQ/DEC/RFC）——**Classification Table Creation REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；不得借 DQ-10 归档任务创建 Audit Schema / Event Schema / Event Registry。DQ-10 不要求新独立 Technical Spike；DQ-07 已要求的真实 PostgreSQL 多 Worker Concurrency Technical Spike 继续有效并应覆盖 Integration Event 重复 Delivery / Consumer Dedup / Relay Crash / stale Event Publish Attempt / 无部分业务写入（Spike 仍需单独明确授权）。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：审计 append-only 同事务原子写、Application Event 提交后通知（[架构推断]）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + 六类独立语义 + Audit 字段与更正语义 + State Transition Record + Integration Event Outbox + CloudEvents 边界 + Observability 边界 + 所有权边界 + 非 Event Sourcing + Classification Table 前置；Candidate B Universal Event / Audit Table 拒绝；Candidate C 全项目架构拒绝）。**不采用完整 Event Sourcing**（与 DEC-013 一致；DQ-10 Accepted Decision 已明确 Audit Ledger / State Transition Record / Integration Event Outbox 均不构成 Business Current Truth；快照与历史模型已由 **DQ-11 Accepted Decision（2026-08-02）** 正式决定：Candidate A 重大修订——Authoritative Current Truth + Immutable Business Version Snapshots + Append-only Audit/Transition History + Optional Derived Query Projections，Full Event Sourcing / Current-state-only Overwrite / Delta-only 权威历史拒绝，见第 9 条与 §33）。
9. **快照与历史模型 = Authoritative Current Truth + Immutable Business Version Snapshots + Append-only Audit/State Transition History + Optional Derived Query Projections（DQ-11 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A）**：**① Formal Model 与三类拒绝**：PostgreSQL Business Current Truth 是唯一业务权威来源，Current Truth = Logical Business Object + Explicit Current Truth Pointer + Selected Immutable Business Version；Audit Record / State Transition Record / Domain Event / Integration Event / Workflow Checkpoint / Observability Event / Query Projection 均不构成 Business Current Truth；**不采用完整 Event Sourcing（Candidate B，违反 DEC-013）、不采用仅覆盖 Current State 丢失版本历史的模型（Candidate C，违反 DEC-024）、Delta-only Version History 不作为权威 MVP 历史模型**；系统不得重放 Audit / State Transition / Domain / Application / Integration / Observability Event / Durable Work Intent / Workflow Checkpoint 重建权威 Current Truth。**② Version Boundary**：每个需历史/比较/审查/恢复/可追溯性的业务对象有明确 Version Boundary（遵守 DQ-03 Aggregate Boundary 与 DQ-02 唯一模块所有权；不得创建跨模块 Task Mega Snapshot；一个 Business Version 默认属于一个 Logical Business Object；跨 Aggregate Composite Commit 可同事务创建多个独立版本——各自保留 logical object identity / domain version identity / version number / Current Truth Pointer / Aggregate invariants，经 Command ID / Correlation / Causation / Commit Identity 建立因果但不合并为 Mega Snapshot）。**③ Immutable Business Version Snapshot**：使用 DQ-04 的 `logical_object_id` / `domain_version_id`（不可变不复用）/ `version_number`（同对象内单调递增、已提交 Version Number 不因 Invalidation/Rejection/Supersession/Restore/Archive/Retention 复用）；正式版本业务语义不可变（普通 Application Command 不得 UPDATE 已提交版本业务内容、不得 DELETE 历史版本；修正必须创建新版本；Invalidation/Rejection/Supersession/Restore 均保留来源版本）；每个正式版本至少包含或等价表达 Snapshot Schema Version / Logical Object Identity / Domain Version Identity / Version Number / Creation Command Identity / Creation Actor / Created Time / Recorded Time / Business Content / Immutable Dependency References / Status or Validity / Provenance / Causation-Parent-Derived-from。**④ Logical Completeness**：正式版本逻辑完整、独立可读（读取历史版本无需从首版本重放全部 Event/Delta；Delta-only Chain 不作权威历史读取路径；不得要求 V1 full + V2~V4 delta 才能理解 V4）；逻辑完整可物理分布在 Version Root / Version-owned Child Rows / Immutable Value Objects / Immutable Source-Evidence-External References / Version-owned Association Rows（不要求单 JSONB 字段，物理规范化不得破坏版本语义独立性）；读取历史版本不得依赖后来变化的 Current Truth Pointer / `current_source_version` / `current_dependency` 等可变引用，影响历史语义的依赖必须固定到 Source/Evidence/Dependency Version Identity / Content Hash / Prompt-Model-Config Version Reference 等不可变引用。**⑤ Version Creation Boundary**：仅成功完成的正式 Atomic Business Commit 产生正式 Domain Version（Database/40001/40P01 Retry、Work Execution/Provider Retry、Worker Crash Recovery、Lease Renewal、Heartbeat、Checkpoint Save、Workflow Resume 本身、Observability Update、Telemetry Export、最终回滚事务、未提交 LLM 临时输出、草稿中间结果、仅状态探测/健康检查均不得产生新正式版本）；Intentional Rerun 成功后可产生新版本；仅改变 Lease Holder / Attempt ID / Retry Counter / Heartbeat / Worker Runtime State / Checkpoint Position / Telemetry Metadata 等运行状态不得创建版本；Audit Record 产生不必然意味着创建新 Domain Version；Version Creation Policy 由 Application 层显式决定（不得仅依赖 ORM Dirty Tracking / SQLAlchemy Flush）。**⑥ Current Truth Pointer**：可变的权威当前版本选择器，拥有独立非空 `revision`、修改必须携带 `expected_revision`、使用 DQ-04 Compare-and-Swap 条件更新（CAS 零行 = 冲突并回滚完整 Atomic Business Commit）；一个明确 Business Scope 最多一个 Current Effective Version，可显式处于 `NO_CURRENT_TRUTH`；Pointer 不得指向 Invalid / Rejected / 物理不可用 / 非同一 Logical Business Object / 未完成正式 Commit / 被安全策略禁止的版本；**`MAX(version_number)` 不得用作 Current Truth Selector、`ORDER BY version_number DESC LIMIT 1` 不得隐式等同 Current Truth 查询**，`latest_created_version` / `latest_approved_version` / `current_effective_version` 三者独立命名与查询语义、Query/Repository/API/Application Contract 不得混用。**⑦ Promotion**：已正式创建但尚未 Current Truth 的不可变 Candidate Version 可经显式 Promotion Command 成为 Current Truth（重新验证 Candidate 状态 / Business Invariants / expected revision / Evidence-Source Validity / Review Decision / Security Constraints / Current Concurrency Ownership / 所属关系），与 Current Truth Pointer / Stage State / Audit / State Transition / Idempotency / Integration Event Outbox / Durable Work Intent 等适用参与者同一 PostgreSQL Atomic Business Commit，且不得修改被 Promote 的不可变 Version Content。**⑧ Invalidation**：Current Version 被 Invalidate 后不得继续作为 Current Truth；**Invalidation 不删除原版本、不得自动 select previous maximum valid version、不得静默回退**至前一 Version Number / 最近 Approved / 任意仍标 Valid / 任何未重新验证的版本；Invalidation 后必须显式选择创建 Replacement / Promote 已审查 Candidate / 执行 Restore / 进入 `NO_CURRENT_TRUTH` / 或其他明确业务不变量允许的状态；Invalidation 追加 Audit 与 State Transition Evidence，且与 Current Truth Pointer 修改使用 CAS。**⑨ Restore**：将历史内容恢复为当前业务状态必须执行显式 Restore Command——**Restore 是新的前向 Business Operation，不是 Database Transaction Rollback**；必须创建新 `domain_version_id` / `version_number` / `command_id` / audit record，并记录 `restored_from_version_id` / restore reason / restored by / restore command identity / restore time / 当前验证通过的 Source-Evidence references；不得原地修改被引用历史版本、不得简单把 Pointer 重新指向曾被 Supersede/Invalidate/失效的旧版本；必须重新验证当前 Business Rules / Source-Evidence Validity / Security-Permission / Review Requirements / Current Schema Compatibility / expected revision / 并发状态 / 当前依赖有效性；Restore 作为新 Atomic Business Commit，新版本内容可等价历史版本但必须有新 Version Identity 与新业务因果记录；**Database Rollback 与 Business Restore 必须使用不同术语、不同 Port、不同 Application Contract 与不同测试**。**⑩ Mutable Query Projection**：可用于提升读取/列表/搜索/聚合性能，**默认派生数据、不是 Business Current Truth**（可位于同一 PostgreSQL 服务，不强制 CQRS / 独立 Read Store；可从 Current Truth Pointer 与 Version Tables 构建、不要求 Event Stream 重放）；Projection 延迟/损坏/丢失/重建不得改变 Business Current Truth；State-changing Command 不得只依赖可能过期 Projection 决定最终 Commit（用 Projection 候选筛选时最终 Commit 前必须重新读取并验证权威 Current Truth）；Projection 必须有明确 Owning Module / Source of Truth / Rebuild Strategy / Consistency Level / Allowed Query Use / Failure Handling / Freshness Expectation；Projection Rebuild 不得创建新 Domain Version。**⑪ 历史读取语义**：系统至少支持 Read Current Truth / Read Version by `domain_version_id` / Read Version by `version_number` / List Version History / Compare Two Versions / Read Audit Timeline / Read State Transition Timeline / Request Explicit Restore；历史版本读取返回当时正式提交语义（后续 Current Truth / Source-Dependency Current Pointer / Projection 变化不得改变已提交历史版本展示结果）；Version History 用 `version_number` 表达同对象版本顺序（Timestamp 不作唯一 Version Identity 或唯一排序依据）；Audit Timeline 用 DQ-10 `occurred_at` / `recorded_at` / stable Audit Identity 表达审计顺序（可解释状态如何变化，但不得作为重建 Current Truth 权威输入）；Version Compare 必须比较两个不可变 Business Version Snapshot（不得比较两个时间点可变 Projection 并称为历史版本比较）；API 路径/分页/Diff Response/Restore Endpoint/Authorization/用户可见性留 RFC-004。**⑫ Temporal / Bitemporal Boundary**：DQ-11 不在 MVP 引入通用 Bitemporal Database Model（Application Time 与 System/Recorded Time 是不同概念，仅有明确业务需求才为具体对象建模 Application Time；不要求每表 `valid_from`/`valid_to`/`system_from`/`system_to`、不要求全项目通用 SQL `AS OF` 查询层、不要求数据库系统版本时间自动生成业务历史；普通 `created_at` 不得当作完整 Application Time / Effective Time / Bitemporal Model）。**⑬ Atomic Commit 与并发**：新 Domain Version 创建与所有适用参与者（Evidence Links / Current Truth Pointer / Stage State / Audit / State Transition / Idempotency / Integration Event Outbox / Durable Work Intent / Result Reference）同一 DEC-035 Atomic Business Commit；Version Number 分配与 Version Insert 同事务、Version Insert 与 Current Truth Pointer CAS 同事务、CAS 失败整体回滚；冲突/失败事务不得留下孤立 Domain Version / 已更新 Pointer / 已完成 Stage State / 成功 Audit / 成功 State Transition / 成功 Idempotency / 可发布 Integration Event / 可领取 Durable Work Intent / 部分 Evidence Links；数据库事务 Retry 复用同一 Logical Command Identity、不得创建额外正式版本；并发 Version Creation 经唯一约束与 CAS 防重复 Version Number / 重复 Domain Version Identity / 静默覆盖 Current Truth / 孤立 Version。**⑭ Retention 与删除**：正常业务流程不得物理删除历史 Business Version（Invalidation/Rejection/Supersession/Promotion/Restore 均不删除旧版本）；Archive/Cold Storage/Physical Deletion/Legal Hold/法规删除留 DQ-15，PII/Encryption/Redaction/Access Control/删除例外留 DQ-17（DQ-15/17 决定前不得假设历史版本可短期删除）。**⑮ Snapshot 术语边界**：`Business Version Snapshot`（不可变业务版本）/ `Workflow Checkpoint`（Runtime 恢复状态）/ `Database Backup Snapshot`（基础设施灾难恢复副本）/ `Event-Sourcing Snapshot`（Event Stream 重放优化）/ `Query Projection`（派生读取模型）五种概念语义独立；Business Version Snapshot 不得被 LangGraph Checkpoint 替代、Workflow Checkpoint 不作 Business Version History、Database Backup 不作可查询业务历史、Query Projection 不作不可变历史证据、本项目不使用 Event-Sourcing Snapshot。**⑯ 实现前置条件**：DQ-11 不新增独立 Matrix；已要求的 **Aggregate / Invariant Matrix 未来获得授权时必须扩展** Current Truth Owner / Versioned Object / Version Boundary / Version Creation Trigger / Snapshot Granularity / Logical Completeness Rule / Current Truth Selector / Promotion Rule / Invalidation Rule / Restore Rule / Historical Dependency References / Projection Source of Truth / Retention Owner / Related DQ-DEC-RFC——**DQ-11 接受不授权创建或修改 Aggregate / Invariant Matrix**；DQ-11 不要求新独立 Technical Spike，已要求的真实 PostgreSQL Concurrency Technical Spike 未来获得授权后必须覆盖 Concurrent Version Number Allocation / Current Truth Pointer CAS / Current Version Invalidation / Promotion-versus-Invalidation / Restore-versus-New Write / Concurrent Restore / No Orphan Version / No Partial Atomic Commit；本次归档不创建 Spike Issue/Branch/PR/代码/测试/基础设施。测试前置语义：所有正式 Snapshot/History 事务语义测试使用真实 PostgreSQL，后续测试至少覆盖 20 项（见 DQ-11 第 157 点）；详细测试分类与 CI 策略留 DQ-16。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：current truth + 版本化历史 + append-only 审计、不采用完整 ES（[架构推断]）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + Formal Model 四要素 + 三类拒绝 + Version Boundary + Immutable Snapshot + Logical Completeness + Version Creation Boundary + Current Truth Pointer + Promotion/Invalidation/Restore + Query Projection + 历史读取语义 + Bitemporal 边界 + Atomic Commit 并发 + Retention 边界 + Snapshot 术语 + Matrix 扩展/Spike 关系；Candidate B 完整 ES 拒绝；Candidate C 仅当前状态覆盖拒绝；Delta-only 权威历史拒绝）。
10. **来源与证据持久化 = PostgreSQL Authoritative Source/Evidence Graph + Immutable Content-addressed Source Blobs + Versioned Derived Artifacts and Fragments + Explicit Evidence-to-Claim Links + Rebuildable Non-authoritative Retrieval Index（DQ-12 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A）**：**① Formal Model 与两类拒绝**：PostgreSQL 是 Source/Evidence 业务身份、状态、关系、Provenance、Fragment 和 Evidence Link 的唯一权威来源；对象存储只保存符合外部存储策略的不可变内容字节、不是 Business Current Truth；Retrieval Index 不是 Business Current Truth；Search Result / Vector ID / Embedding / Ranking Score 均不是正式 Evidence；**Candidate B（全部内容无条件存 PostgreSQL 通用策略）与 Candidate C（全部内容无条件存对象存储仅 DB 引用通用策略）均拒绝**（不表示 PostgreSQL 不能存中小文本/规范化内容/Fragment/结构化元数据，也不表示对象存储不能存大型/二进制/流式内容）；PostgreSQL Large Object 不作为 MVP 默认存储路径。**② 核心身份分离**：Source（逻辑来源，不等于一次抓取结果）/ SourceVersion（一次正式捕获的不可变内容状态，稳定不可复用身份、不得被后续抓取覆盖）/ ContentObject（实际物理字节）/ Acquisition（一次获取尝试，失败下载/超时/认证失败/Parser Failure/Checksum Failure 不得产生正式 SourceVersion）/ DerivedArtifact（版本化派生产物）/ Fragment / FragmentSet / EvidenceLink（显式业务关系，不等于 Retrieval 命中）/ RetrievalChunk / RetrievalIndexEntry 必须明确区分，不得通过通用 `document_id`/`source_id` 或无类型 JSON 隐式合并；相同物理字节可复用 ContentObject 但不得自动合并不同 SourceVersion（是否创建新 SourceVersion 由显式 Application Policy 决定，不得仅依据 Content Hash 合并不同来源/时间/权限/Provenance/法律要求）；历史 Business Version 与 EvidenceLink 不得经 Source Current Pointer 解析内容、必须固定到不可变 SourceVersion。**③ PostgreSQL 权威数据**：保存 Source/SourceVersion/Acquisition/ContentObject Metadata/Hash Algorithm+Hash+Byte Length+Media Type/Encoding/Storage Classification/Object Reference/Integrity+Availability State/Provenance/DerivedArtifact/Fragment/FragmentSet/EvidenceLink/Current Pointer/Revision/Audit References，并判断哪个 SourceVersion 正式存在、哪个 ContentObject 已验证、Fragment 归属、EvidenceLink 支持关系、Integrity Incident；对象存储与 Retrieval Index 均不得成为权威来源。**④ Storage Classification Policy**：依显式策略决定（考虑 Size/Media Type/Streaming/Backup-Restore Impact/Retention/Security/Legal Hold/Cost 等），不硬编码任意字节阈值；默认存 PostgreSQL = 结构化元数据/中小原始文本/规范化文本/Parsed Data/Canonical Fragment/EvidenceLink/小型 JSONB；默认考虑外部对象存储 = 大型原始文档/二进制/图片/音频/视频/Archive/流式/对备份影响显著的内容；**TOAST 是透明物理机制、不作业务存储策略或大小分界；PostgreSQL Large Object 不作默认 MVP 路径**。**⑤ Raw 与 Derived 分离**：Raw Source Content 不可变（不得被 Parser/Normalizer/OCR/Chunking 原地覆盖）；Normalized/Parsed/OCR 均为版本化 DerivedArtifact；处理规则变化必须产生新 DerivedArtifact Version 或 FragmentSet、不覆盖旧结果；DerivedArtifact 保留完整 Derivation Chain、不得静默引用输入 Source 最新版本。**⑥ Content Hash**：每个 ContentObject 至少记录 Hash Algorithm + Content Hash + Byte Length + Media Type（不得只存无算法身份的通用 `checksum`）；Raw Hash 与 Normalized Hash 分别计算、不得混用；**对象存储 ETag 不得作为项目权威 Content Hash**；Hash 相同只证明字节一致，不证明 Source/Provenance/权限/法律状态/Evidence 含义相同。**⑦ Content-addressed Object Storage**：外部 Blob 优先内容寻址（推荐 Key 形态 `<algorithm>/<content-hash>`）；写入必须 Put-if-absent/Conditional Put，不得无条件覆盖；同 Key 不同字节视为严重完整性冲突；Object Key 不得暴露原始文件名/完整 URL/User Name/Tenant Identity/Token/Secret/不必要 PII。**⑧ 一致性协议**：**不假设 PostgreSQL 与对象存储统一分布式 ACID 事务、不采用 XA**；采用 **Prepare Content → Upload Immutable Object → Verify Checksum/Presence → Finalize Metadata in Short PostgreSQL Transaction**；**PostgreSQL 不得提交指向未验证 Object 的正式 SourceVersion、不得先提交 Active SourceVersion 再异步上传**；Finalization 失败不得产生正式 Current Truth/EvidenceLink/SourceVersion。**⑨ Orphan Object**：Upload 成功但 DB Commit 失败只产生未引用 Orphan（不等于正式 ContentObject、不得自动成为 SourceVersion、必须可经 Reconciliation 发现；Reconciler 仅在 Grace Period 与引用检查后处理、不得删除仍被引用的 Object、不得依据单次最终一致读取立即删除；Grace Period 留 DQ-15）。**⑩ Missing/Corrupt Object = Integrity Incident**：已正式引用 Object 缺失或 Checksum 不匹配不得静默忽略/重抓 URL 最新内容替换/切换 SourceVersion/改历史记录；必须导致状态更新 + Audit/Incident Record + 告警 + 依赖分析 + 必要 Review；受影响 Business Version 不得自动改绑新 SourceVersion。**⑪ Acquisition / Provenance**：Acquisition 与 SourceVersion 独立（失败 Acquisition 不创建正式 SourceVersion，Retry 遵守 DQ-08 幂等身份）；Provenance 表达 Entity/Activity/Agent 并记录 derived_from/processor identity+version/acquisition method/locator/Content Hash/Correlation-Causation Identity，不得含未脱敏 Secret 或不必要 PII。**⑫ Fragment**：必须属于明确 SourceVersion 或 DerivedArtifact Version（不得只属于可变 Source）；**Offset Unit 必须显式**（UTF-8 Byte Offset/Unicode Code Point/PDF Page Coordinate/Timecode/DOM Selector/Section Selector 等），不得只存无法解释的 `start=100`/`end=200`；Parser/OCR/Chunking/Segmentation Profile 变化必须创建新 FragmentSet 且不覆盖旧 Fragment。**⑬ Canonical Fragment / RetrievalChunk**：EvidenceLink 默认引用 SourceVersion + Canonical Fragment 或 Typed Selector，RetrievalIndexEntry 默认引用 RetrievalChunk；RetrievalChunk 必须能回链 Canonical Fragment/SourceVersion/DerivedArtifact；**EvidenceLink 不得只引用 Vector ID**；重新 Chunk/Embedding/更换 Vector Store 不得破坏既有 EvidenceLink。**⑭ EvidenceLink**：显式业务关系（Relation Type 可表达 SUPPORTS/CONTRADICTS/QUALIFIES/CONTEXT/PRIMARY_SOURCE/DERIVED_FROM）；**必须指向不可变 SourceVersion，不得指向 Source Current Pointer/URL 最新内容/Pending SourceVersion/Failed Acquisition/未验证 ContentObject/仅 Vector ID 的 Retrieval Entry**；不得因 Source Current Pointer 变化改变历史含义；创建/Invalidation/Supersession 必须可审计。**⑮ Evidence Atomicity**：正式 Business Version 依赖 Evidence 时，Business Version + Evidence Links + Current Truth Pointer + Stage State + Audit + State Transition + Idempotency + Integration Event Outbox + Durable Work Intent + Result Reference 等适用参与者同一 DEC-035 PostgreSQL Atomic Business Commit；外部 Blob 必须在最终事务前验证完成；EvidenceLink 写入失败则 Business Commit 整体回滚、不留已生效 EvidenceLink/Current Truth；Database Retry 不得重新上传已验证 Blob、复用同一逻辑 Command 与 Content Identity。**⑯ Evidence Invalidation**：SourceVersion/ContentObject/DerivedArtifact/Fragment 被判定 Corrupt/Unavailable/Retracted/Superseded/Unsafe/Legally Restricted/Integrity Failed 时不得改写历史 EvidenceLink 或静默改绑 Source 最新版本（经新状态/Invalidation Record/Audit/State Transition/Review Workflow 表达；Invalidation Does Not Mean Deletion 继续有效）。**⑰ Retrieval Index（RFC-005）**：派生/可重建/非权威；Index Entry 至少回链 SourceVersion ID/Fragment ID/Index Generation/Chunking Profile Version/Embedding Model-Version/Input Content Hash；正式 Evidence Commit 前必须解析回 PostgreSQL SourceVersion/Fragment/Typed Selector 并验证 Identity/Hash/Availability/Integrity/Evidence Policy/Business Invariants；Index 延迟/损坏/重建不得改变 Current Truth 或既有 EvidenceLink。**⑱ Deduplication**：ContentObject 可按项目 Content Hash 物理去重，但不得合并 Source/SourceVersion/Acquisition/Provenance/Access Control/EvidenceLink/Retention/Legal Hold/Security Classification；**跨 Tenant 或跨 Security Domain 物理去重在 DQ-17 决定前不得启用**。**⑲ Retention 与安全边界**：Retention/Archive/Legal Hold/Physical Deletion/Orphan Grace Period 留 DQ-15；Encryption/Redaction/Secret/PII/Access Control/Object Key Security/跨域去重留 DQ-17；Source/Evidence Metadata 与 ContentObject 必须能分别应用安全策略。**⑳ 所有权与前置条件**：Source/Evidence Capability 是 Source/SourceVersion/Acquisition/ContentObject Metadata/DerivedArtifact/Fragment/FragmentSet/EvidenceLink/Object Storage Adapter/Integrity Reconciliation 的唯一所有者（业务模块不得直接访问 ORM/表/Repository/Object Key 或绕过 SDK）；**Source & Evidence Storage Classification Table 为持久化实现前置 Architecture Readiness Package 必备——REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；**External Object Consistency Technical Spike（Conditional Put/同 Hash 并发/同 Key 冲突/Multipart Checksum/Orphan Reconciliation/Missing-Corrupt 检测/真实 Provider Read-after-write/无部分 Current Truth 写入）为外部对象存储实现前置——REQUIRED BEFORE EXTERNAL STORAGE IMPLEMENTATION 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；DQ-12 不新增独立 Matrix、不选择 Provider；所有正式事务语义测试使用真实 PostgreSQL，22 项最低覆盖清单见 DQ-12 第 227 点，详细测试分类与 CI 策略留 DQ-16。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：DB 存中小原始内容与全部证据元数据/链接、特大/二进制走外部对象存储 + 引用（[架构推断]）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + Formal Model 五要素 + 身份分离 + PostgreSQL 权威 + Storage Classification + Raw/Derived 分离 + Content Hash + Content-addressed Object Storage + Upload/Verify/Finalize 协议 + Orphan/Integrity Incident + Provenance + Fragment/EvidenceLink + Retrieval Index 边界 + Dedup/Retention/Security 边界 + 所有权 + Classification Table/Spike 前置；Candidate B/C 通用策略拒绝）。
11. **Workflow Checkpoint 分离 = Shared PostgreSQL Service + Isolated Checkpoint Persistence Plane + Dedicated Role/Connection Pool/Storage Namespace + Application-owned Workflow Execution Registry + Business-Current-Truth-first Reconciliation（DQ-13 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A）**：**① Formal Model 与候选处置**：MVP 采用 Shared PostgreSQL Service；Candidate B（独立 PostgreSQL 服务/独立基础设施）不作 MVP（同 Service 内独立 Checkpoint Database 是 Candidate A 的安全 Fallback，不等于 Candidate B）；Candidate C 同表混存拒绝（Business 与 Vendor Checkpoint Record 不得同业务表）。**② 三个持久化平面**：Business Persistence Plane（Current Truth/Immutable Business Version/Current Truth Pointer/Audit/State Transition/Evidence/Idempotency/Durable Work Intent/Integration Event Outbox 等 DEC-035 参与者）/ Application-owned Workflow Execution Registry（项目自有 Runtime/Application 记录，≠Business Current Truth，是 Workflow Run↔Thread 映射权威来源）/ Vendor Checkpoint Persistence Plane（LangGraph Checkpointer 管理，只保存 Graph 恢复状态/Blob/Pending Writes/Vendor Migration Metadata）必须区分，不得经通用 Workflow 表/State JSON/ORM Model 合并；业务模块不得直接查询或修改 Vendor Checkpoint Tables。**③ 物理放置**：优先形态 = 同 Database + Dedicated Checkpoint Schema，**但必须用项目实际钉定的 Python PostgresSaver + Psycopg Pool + 部署 Pooler 证明 Setup 与全部运行时 SQL（Put/Get/List/Delete）落预期 Schema、Pool Reuse 后 search_path 不漂移、Worker 并发 Namespace 不漂移、Pooler Transaction/Session Mode 不破坏 Schema Resolution**（不得仅凭文档推测；无法稳定保证则用同 Service 内 Dedicated Checkpoint Database Fallback，须独立 Credentials/Pool/Migration Lifecycle）；不选择具体 Pooler、不授权创建 Database 或 Schema。**④ Role 与 Connection Pool**：独立 Business Pool 与 Checkpoint Pool（互不混用、Checkpoint Connection 不进 Application UoW、Checkpoint Role 最小权限不拥有 Business DDL/UPDATE/DELETE/Migration Owner/Audit/Source-Evidence/Current Truth 修改权）、Business Role 不直接修改 Vendor Tables、Migration Role 与 Runtime Checkpoint Role 可分离、Checkpoint Pool 独立 Pool Size/Timeout/Health Check/Retry/Observability/Credential Rotation；Worker 长执行不持 Business Session/UoW，Checkpointer 短数据库操作不包装进业务 UoW。**⑤ Workflow Execution Registry**：明确 Application Contract（至少 workflow_run_id/thread_id/checkpoint_namespace/command_id/stage_run_id/Target Business Type+Identity/Base Domain Version/Expected Revision/Input Fingerprint/Runtime Lifecycle/Reconciliation Status/Graph Definition Version/Checkpoint Schema Version/Serializer Version/Stale-Superseded Reason/Created-Updated Time/Retention Eligibility/Related Dispatch ID/Current Attempt ID）；workflow_run_id 是项目自有逻辑 Workflow Execution Identity、thread_id 是 Framework Checkpoint Identity、一个 Workflow Run 显式映射 Thread（不得解析 thread_id 字符串推断）；Registry 不保存 Session/Repository/Vendor Connection。**⑥ 身份分离**：workflow_run_id/thread_id/checkpoint_id/checkpoint_namespace/command_id/stage_run_id/attempt_id/dispatch_id/domain_version_id/Idempotency Key/Provider Call Identity 必须分离（thread_id 不得作 Command ID/Stage Run ID/Idempotency Key/Lease Identity；checkpoint_id 不得作 Domain Version ID 或业务成功证明；Checkpoint 存在不表 Business Commit 成功、Checkpoint 缺失不表业务未发生；Attempt Identity 不替代 Workflow Run Identity；Intentional Rerun 创建新 Workflow Run Identity 与 Thread Identity）。**⑦ Checkpoint 权威边界**：Checkpoint 是 Runtime Recovery State，≠Business Current Truth/Business Version/Audit/State Transition/Idempotency/Work Intent/Integration Event/EvidenceLink/Review Decision/Provider Call Ledger/执行锁；Checkpoint 中复制业务字段仅恢复上下文、不覆盖 Current Truth、不回答权威业务问题；Business Current Truth 变化时 Checkpoint 让步。**⑧ Payload 边界**：只保存恢复 Graph Execution 最小 Runtime State（Workflow Run Identity/Command-Stage Run Reference/Base Domain Version/Expected Revision/Input Fingerprint/Node Cursor/Interrupt Payload/Tool-Node Result Reference/Pending Runtime Writes/Graph Definition Version/Checkpoint Schema Version/Reconciliation Metadata/Runtime-only Channel Values），不得保存 Session/UoW/Repository/ORM Entity/Connection/Coroutine/Generator/未脱敏 Secret/长期 Provider Token/长期 Presigned URL/不必要完整 PII/数据库整行无选择复制/不受控任意 Python Object；Provider 响应存受控 Snapshot 或不可变 Reference；Checkpoint State 不替代 DQ-12 Source/Evidence 持久化。**⑨ Serializer 与安全**：明确 Allowlist（不得任意 Python Class 反序列化、Checkpoint 视为不完全可信持久化输入）、Resume 前验证 Serializer Version/Payload Schema/Graph Definition Compatibility/Integrity Metadata、不兼容/无法安全反序列化 Payload 分类 INCOMPATIBLE/CORRUPT；Encryption/Redaction/PII/访问控制留 DQ-17（决定前不假设 Payload 明文长期保存）、Runtime Log 不输出完整 Payload/Secret/长期访问 URL。**⑩ Business Commit 与 Checkpoint Write**：**不构成同一 Atomic Transaction**（同一 PG Service 也不得声称同一 Application UoW，Vendor Checkpoint Connection 不属 DEC-035 Atomic Business Commit）；四类 Crash Window——A.Checkpoint 成功+Business Commit 未发生（Resume 重新验证 Current Truth，不推断业务已提交）；B.Checkpoint 成功+Business Commit 回滚（经 Reconciliation 识别业务未提交）；C.Business Commit 成功+Checkpoint Write 失败（Business Commit 仍有效，Resume 经 DQ-08 幂等+Current Truth 识别已完成操作，不重调 Provider/不重复副作用）；D.两者都成功（Resume 仍执行 Reconciliation，Durability 与 Consistency 互补非同一事务）。**⑪ Resume Reconciliation**：每次 Resume/Retry/Human Interrupt 恢复/Time Travel Fork 前执行 Load Registry→Load Checkpoint→Load Current Truth→Validate Identity-Version→Classify→Resume or Refuse；至少验证 Workflow Run ID/Thread ID/Command ID/Stage Run ID/Input Fingerprint/Base-Current Domain Version/Expected Revision/Current Stage State/Idempotency Result/Lease Holder/Attempt ID/Fencing Token/Graph Definition Version/Checkpoint Schema Version/Serializer Version/Workflow Runtime Status；Reconciliation 至少区分 RESUMABLE/ALREADY_COMMITTED/STALE/SUPERSEDED/INVALIDATED/ORPHANED/INCOMPATIBLE/CORRUPT（只有 RESUMABLE 可继续原 Thread；ALREADY_COMMITTED 不重复副作用只重放结果或推进 Completion；STALE 不覆盖新 Current Truth；非 RESUMABLE 停止原 Thread Resume）。**⑫ Stale/Superseded**：不得静默修改 Checkpoint Base Domain Version、不得把 Retry 伪装成 Resume、不得复用旧 Command Identity 创建新逻辑操作；允许标记 STALE/SUPERSEDED、终止当前 Attempt、保存诊断 Metadata、按 DQ-15 保留或删除、启动 Intentional Rerun（创建新 Command ID/Stage Run ID/Workflow Run ID/Thread ID/Attempt ID/Dispatch ID/Provider Call Identity 并保留父级因果关系）。**⑬ 并发控制**：Checkpoint 不承担并发控制（Thread ID/Checkpoint ID/Checkpoint Record/Write Success/Graph State 布尔 Flag 均非执行锁）；执行所有权继续由 DQ-07 Lease+Lease Holder+Attempt ID+单调 Fencing Token 提供；同一 Workflow Run/Thread 并发 Resume 拒绝第二个或经 Lease 串行化、不依赖 Checkpointer 自动阻止；最终 Business Commit 重新验证 Fencing Token；Lease 过期/Fencing Token 失效后旧 Worker 不得提交、Checkpoint 写入成功不恢复执行权。**⑭ Durability Mode**：逐节点策略留 RFC-003（exit 禁用于 Human-in-the-loop/Awaiting Review/长时间等待/需故障恢复生产流程/跨多节点正式生产工作流；async 仅在可容忍最近恢复点丢失+副作用受 DQ-08 幂等保护+必须执行工作由 DQ-09 Work Intent 持久化+Business Commit 可独立对账+重算可接受时允许；sync 是 Interrupt 前/Human Review 等待前/不可廉价重算步骤后/Provider Side-effect Result 记录后/正式 Business Commit 相关边界/Runtime 释放 Lease 前默认安全方向；sync ≠ Business Commit 与 Checkpoint Atomic）。**⑮ Time Travel/Fork**：Graph Time Travel ≠ Business Restore（从旧 Checkpoint Fork 不自动覆盖 Current Truth、不自动复用旧 Command Identity、Debug Fork 非正式业务结果；Fork 入正式状态须转 Intentional Rerun 创建新身份+新 DEC-035 Atomic Business Commit；DQ-11 Business Restore 继续创建新 Domain Version，Checkpoint Fork 不替代 Restore Command）。**⑯ Setup 与 Vendor Migration**：PostgresSaver Setup 属 Vendor Schema 初始化迁移，生产 Worker 启动不得无条件并发执行 Setup DDL（经受控部署步骤/Migration Job/单一受控初始化机制）；langgraph-checkpoint-postgres 版本必须钉定；Vendor Checkpoint Migration 与 Business Alembic Migration 必须分离（Business Alembic 不管理 Vendor Tables、Runtime Module 不自行修改 Vendor Schema）；Package 升级前验证 Schema Migration/Checkpoint Compatibility/Resume Compatibility/Serializer Compatibility/Rollout-Rollback Boundary/Setup Idempotency/Pool-Search Path Compatibility；DQ-14 管理总体 Migration Discipline 但不把 Vendor Table 纳入普通业务 Migration Ownership。**⑰ Retention 与删除**：Checkpoint 是可清理 Runtime Artifact（可清理 ≠ 任意时间删除；仅 Terminal/无有效 Lease/无 Pending Resume/无 Awaiting Human Input/无 Retry Window/无 Incident Hold/无 Debug Hold/无 Legal Hold/Registry 标记可清理的 Thread 可进入删除，不得删 ACTIVE/IN_PROGRESS/INTERRUPTED/AWAITING_REVIEW/FAILED_RETRYABLE/RECONCILING/INCIDENT_INVESTIGATION 状态）；删除 Checkpoint 不删除或改变 Current Truth/Audit/State Transition/Domain Version/Evidence/Work Intent/Integration Event/Idempotency Result/Provider Call Ledger；默认 Whole-thread Lifecycle Deletion（未验证 Package Version/Checkpoint Chain/DeltaChannel 完整性禁止 Partial Pruning，不假设任意 PostgresSaver 版本支持安全 Partial Pruning）；Retention Period/Grace Period/Legal Hold/Physical Cleanup 留 DQ-15；删除必须可审计。**⑱ 所有权与前置条件**：Workflow Runtime Capability 拥有 Registry/Reconciliation Application Contract/Checkpoint Adapter/Runtime Lifecycle/Checkpoint Cleanup Coordination；Vendor Checkpoint Tables 由 Checkpointer Package 存储协议管理（Business Module 不拥有、不直接用 PostgresSaver Repository 或 SQL）；Runtime Module 不直接修改业务 Current Truth（经正式 Business Application Contract）；Reconciliation 读 Current Truth 用业务模块公开 Query/Application Contract；Cross-module Direct SQL 由 DQ-02 禁止；**Workflow Checkpoint Boundary & Reconciliation Table 为持久化实现前置——REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；**Workflow Checkpoint Isolation & Reconciliation Technical Spike（钉定 PostgresSaver/Schema-Database Isolation/Role/Pool/Setup/Crash Window/并发 Resume/Stale Reconciliation/Serializer/Cleanup）为 Checkpoint 实现前置——REQUIRED BEFORE CHECKPOINT IMPLEMENTATION 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；DQ-13 不新增独立 Matrix；所有正式 Checkpoint 数据库语义测试使用真实 PostgreSQL（22 项最低覆盖清单见 DQ-13 第 187 点），详细测试分类与 CI 策略留 DQ-16。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：同实例独立 schema/表、应用层清理、对账以 Business Current Truth 为权威（[架构推断]，置信度高）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + Formal Model 五要素 + 三平面分离 + 物理放置（Dedicated Schema 必须验证 / Dedicated Database Fallback）+ Role/Pool 分离 + Workflow Execution Registry + 身份分离 + Checkpoint 权威边界 + Payload 边界 + Serializer + Crash Window + Resume Reconciliation + Stale/Superseded + 并发控制 + Durability Mode + Time Travel/Fork + Setup/Vendor Migration + Retention/Whole-thread Delete + 所有权 + Readiness Table/Spike 前置；Candidate B 独立 PostgreSQL 服务拒绝；Candidate C 同表混存拒绝）。
12. **Schema 演进纪律 = Alembic-managed Business Schema Migrations + Single Business Migration Lineage + Forward-recovery-first Production Policy + Expand-Migrate-Contract Rolling Compatibility + Resumable Application-owned Data Backfills + Explicit Destructive/Non-transactional DDL Gates + Separate Vendor Migration Lifecycles（DQ-14 Accepted Decision，2026-08-03，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A）**：**① Formal Model 与候选处置**：Business Schema 使用受治理 Alembic Migration Environment；Candidate B（"所有 Migration 应支持安全 Downgrade" 方向）不作通用生产恢复保证（明确可逆、无数据损失且经真实 PostgreSQL 测试的 Migration 可选择性提供安全 Downgrade）；Candidate C 作为唯一 Migration System 拒绝（受治理 Alembic Revision 内允许人工编写 PostgreSQL SQL，不绕过 Revision Graph/Review/Deployment Gate/Migration History）。**② Migration Ownership**：唯一受治理 Business Alembic Environment，每张 Business Table 唯一所有模块遵循 DQ-02，Migration 执行编排由唯一 Migration Capability/Deployment Pipeline/受控 Migration Job 负责（Web/Background/Workflow Worker 不启动自动 `alembic upgrade head`；生产一次只能一个受控 Business Migration Executor 具有独立身份/权限/日志/审计；Runtime Application Role 不应拥有生产 Schema DDL 权限；Migration Role 与 Runtime Role 分离）。**③ Single Business Migration Lineage**：Merge/Release Gate 必须单一 Business Alembic Head（Multiple Heads 不得进受保护主分支或生产 Release；CI 验证 `alembic heads → exactly one Business Head`；经 Rebase 重新生成未发布 Revision/调整依赖/明确 Alembic Merge Revision 解决，不删除已发布 Revision 或修改已运行 revision/down_revision 伪造历史；PostgresSaver Vendor Migration 不伪装成 Business Alembic Head）。**④ Migration History Immutability**：Revision 一旦在共享环境执行即视为已发布记录，不得修改 Revision ID/down_revision/Upgrade/Downgrade 语义/数据转换/DDL/Classification、不删除或重命名；出错必须创建新 Forward Repair Revision；Migration History Immutability ≠ Business Version Immutability。**⑤ Autogenerate Discipline**：仅作 Candidate Migration Generator（Output ≠ Approved Migration），必经人工 Review（Table/Column Rename/Type/Default/Nullable/Constraint/Index/FK/Enum/Schema/Partition/Data Migration/Lock/Rewrite/Destructive Operation）；Rename 误判 Drop/Add 必须人工修正（不导致数据丢失）；drop_table/drop_column/drop_constraint 默认阻塞，过 Destructive Gate 才入生产；Autogenerate 排除 Vendor Checkpoint Tables/非 Business-owned Schema（include_name/include_object 显式配置，不因对象未出现在 Business Metadata 就判断应删除）。**⑥ Schema Drift Gate**：CI 执行 `alembic check` 识别可检测 Metadata Drift（**PASS 不表示 Migration 安全/在线/可逆/无数据损失/无锁风险/已完成 Backfill/已通过 Compatibility 验证**）；CI 还验证 Revision Graph 有效/Business Head 单一/Revision ID 唯一/Vendor Schema 未进 Business Metadata；不直接修改数据库。**⑦ Forward-recovery-first**：生产恢复不依赖通用 Schema Downgrade（不表示所有 downgrade() 必须为空）；默认恢复顺序 Rollback Compatible Application → Keep Expanded Schema → Apply Forward Repair Migration if Required；Forward Repair 用新 Revision，不修改历史 Revision。**⑧ Rollback 术语分离**：Application Rollback/Schema Downgrade/Forward Repair/Database Restore/PITR/DQ-11 Business Restore 独立语义（Database Restore/PITR ≠ 普通 Alembic Downgrade；Alembic Downgrade ≠ Business Restore）。**⑨ Reversibility Classification**：每 Revision 分类 REVERSIBLE_SCHEMA/FORWARD_FIX_ONLY/DATA_IRREVERSIBLE/NON_TRANSACTIONAL_DDL/DESTRUCTIVE_CONTRACT/VENDOR_MANAGED；FORWARD_FIX_ONLY/DATA_IRREVERSIBLE/DESTRUCTIVE_CONTRACT 不得提供看似成功实际丢数据的虚假 Downgrade。**⑩ Expand–Migrate–Contract**：不兼容变更默认 Expand→Compatible Application→Migrate/Backfill→Verify/Cutover→Contract；**Expand 与 Contract 不得同一次发布完成**；Contract 必须过 Destructive Gate，前证明旧 Application 全部退出+Backfill 完成+验证通过+兼容窗口关闭。**⑪ Resumable Backfill**：大型 Backfill 不入长 Alembic Transaction；独立 backfill_run_id/批次游标/Lease/Attempt/Fencing/进度/验证，分批提交/暂停/恢复/幂等重试；遵循 DQ-07 Lease+Fencing 与 DQ-08 Idempotency。**⑫ Technical Backfill ≠ Business Semantic Change**：会改变 Business Current Truth/Domain Version/Review Decision/Evidence/Business Validity/User-visible Business Meaning/Audit Semantics 的处理必须经正式 Business Application Contract + Audit + 版本化规则（DQ-04/08/10/11/DEC-035）；Migration Role 不绕过业务不变量直接制造新 Current Truth。**⑬ PostgreSQL 低锁策略**：大型表优先 Add Nullable Column（不假设 ADD COLUMN DEFAULT 都 Metadata-only/无锁）；Constraint 优先 NOT VALID→修复→VALIDATE；CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY 独立 Non-transactional Boundary + Invalid Index Recovery；Type Change 默认 Shadow Column+Dual Write+Backfill+Cutover+Contract；每生产 Migration 声明 lock_timeout/statement_timeout/Table Scan/Rewrite/Extra Disk/WAL-Replication Impact/Maintenance Window/Retry Safety。**⑭ Destructive Migration Gate**：Drop Table/Column/Constraint、Type Narrowing、不可逆数据转换、Enum 删除/重建、强制 Unique/Non-null、Partition Drop/Detach、Retention 物理删除、大规模重写经显式 Gate（Owner+Schema Reviewer Approval/Impact Analysis/Compatibility 证明/旧 Application 退出/Backfill Verification/Backup-Recovery/DQ-15 Retention/DQ-17 Security-PII/Recovery Runbook/Deployment Window；Autogenerate 产生 Destructive 不构成 Gate Approval，Merge Approval 不自动构成 Production Gate）。**⑮ Non-transactional DDL Gate**：CREATE/DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY 等必须独立 Revision/Step，失败不自动回滚须验证实际状态。**⑯ Vendor Migration Separation**：Business Alembic Migration ≠ PostgresSaver Vendor Migration ≠ Retrieval Index Rebuild ≠ Object Storage Lifecycle（DQ-13 Vendor Setup/Migration 不属 Business Alembic Lineage；不接管 Vendor Checkpoint Tables）。**⑰ Schema Version Identity Separation**：alembic_revision ≠ domain_version_id/version_number/revision/checkpoint_schema_version/graph_definition_version/event_schema_version/payload_schema_version/source_artifact_schema_version；**Alembic Head ≠ Backfill/Validation/Cutover/Contract 完成**（到达 Head 后仍可能 BACKFILL_PENDING/VALIDATION_PENDING/CUTOVER_PENDING/CONTRACT_NOT_AUTHORIZED；Application 据显式 Compatibility/Feature State 判断，不只检查 Alembic Head）。**⑱ Deployment Protocol**：Preflight→Expand Migration→Deploy Compatible Application→Run Resumable Backfill→Verify Data and Traffic→Switch Read Path→End Compatibility Window→Contract Migration（Web/Worker 不自动执行 Migration；Offline SQL 对应正式 Revision 与 Target 版本；Migration 执行成功 ≠ 整个 Feature Rollout 完成）。**⑲ CI/Test Minimum**：单一 Business Head/alembic check 无可检测 drift/fresh+baseline upgrade/revision graph valid/offline SQL reviewable/destructive gated/vendor schema excluded；标记 Reversible 的 Migration 真实 PostgreSQL 测试 Downgrade/Upgrade Round-trip，不可逆 Migration 不得通过虚假 Downgrade 测试获绿色状态。**⑳ Readiness 与 Spike**：**Schema Migration Compatibility & Risk Table 为持久化实现前置——REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；**Schema Migration Rollout & Recovery Technical Spike（Single Head Gate/Revision Graph/Autogenerate 修正/Rename 不产生 Drop-Add/Vendor Schema Exclusion/Expand Compatibility/Application Rollback 不依赖 Downgrade/Resumable Backfill/Crash Recovery/Lock Timeout/NOT VALID-VALIDATE/CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY/Autocommit Partial Commit Boundary/Invalid Index Recovery/Multiple Head Merge/Destructive Gate/Forward Repair/无 Business Partial Write）为生产 Migration Pipeline 前置——REQUIRED BEFORE PRODUCTION MIGRATION IMPLEMENTATION 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；DQ-14 不新增独立 Matrix、不授权创建 Alembic Environment/Revision/Migration/Backfill Registry-Worker/Migration Job/Deployment Gate/DDL/测试/部署配置、不授权修改 Production Database/Vendor Checkpoint Tables、不授权接受 DQ-15 或后续 DQ；详细测试分类与 CI 策略留 DQ-16。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：forward-only、autogenerate 必经人工 review、破坏性变更显式 gate、expand-contract 滚动兼容、大 backfill 拆独立步骤（[架构推断]，置信度中-高）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + Formal Model 七要素 + Migration Ownership + Single Head + History Immutability + Autogenerate Discipline + Drift Gate + Forward-recovery-first + Rollback 术语分离 + Reversibility Classification + Expand-Migrate-Contract + Resumable Backfill + Technical/Business Backfill 边界 + Add Column/Constraint/Concurrent Index/Type Change 低锁策略 + Lock/资源预算 + Destructive/Non-transactional Gate + Vendor Migration Separation + Schema Version Identity Separation + Deployment Protocol + CI/Test Minimum + Readiness Table/Spike 前置；Candidate B Universal Safe Downgrade 拒绝；Candidate C 唯一 Migration System 拒绝）。
13. **数据保留与删除边界 = Classified Retention & Disposition Policies + Purpose/Legal-basis-driven Retention Clocks + Reference-aware Deletion Eligibility + Legal/Security/Incident Hold Overrides + Normal-lifecycle Immutability for Business History + Governed Exceptional Erasure/Redaction Paths + Idempotent Auditable Purge Orchestration + Separate Primary/Object/Index/Checkpoint/Backup Lifecycles（DQ-15 Accepted Decision，2026-08-03，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A）**：**① Formal Model 与两类拒绝**：每类数据独立定义 Retention Policy；**Candidate B Universal TTL 拒绝（违反数据语义与历史要求）、Candidate C Universal Permanent Retention 拒绝（不可持续且可能与 Retention-limitation 义务冲突）**；不得为整个项目设置通用 TTL、不得假设所有数据永久保留；**本决定不设置任何具体保留天数/月数/年数**（具体期限由未来明确的业务/合规/合同/安全/运营决定提供；无合法具体期限时标记 `PERIOD NOT DECIDED`，不得由实现人员虚构）。**② 生命周期术语分离**：Invalidation / Supersession / Archive / Cold Storage / Access Restriction / Redaction / Pseudonymization / Anonymization / Tombstone / Logical Deletion / Physical Purge / Backup Expiry 语义独立——Invalidation/Supersession/Archive/Access Restriction/Encryption/Soft Delete 均**不**表示 Physical Purge；Pseudonymization 不自动等同不可逆匿名化；Anonymization 以无法合理重新识别为目标（精确法律标准留 DQ-17）；术语不得在数据库字段/API/运营手册/审核报告混用。**③ Retention Policy 分类**：每类数据独立 Policy，至少定义 Data Class / Owning Module / Authoritative-Derived / Business Purpose / Legal-Contractual Basis / Retention Trigger / Retention Clock / Minimum Required Horizon / Maximum Permitted Horizon / Deletion Blockers / Hold Types / Normal Disposition / Exceptional Erasure Action / Storage-plane Treatment / Verification Owner（不得只含一个 TTL 数字；必须区分最短必须保留与最长允许保留）。**④ Retention Clock**：**不得统一使用 `created_at`**——Trigger 包括 Workflow Terminal / Business Relationship End / Superseded / Invalidated / Delivery Completed / Replay Window Closed / Deduplication Window Closed / Last Required / Legal Basis Expired / Incident Closed / Object Became Unreferenced / Contract Ended / Review Completed；每个 Data Class 使用与其语义一致的 Trigger；Checkpoint Retention 不从 Checkpoint Created Time 直接计算；Idempotency 覆盖合法 Retry/Replay 风险；Consumer Dedup 覆盖重复投递窗口；Orphan Blob 从确认无正式引用且 Grace Period 开始计算；Business Version 可删除性不仅由年龄判断。**⑤ Business Current Truth**：正常生命周期不被普通 Purge Worker 直接物理删除（业务删除先经 INACTIVE / INVALIDATED / WITHDRAWN / NO_CURRENT_TRUTH 等明确业务状态；普通 Business Command 不绕过业务不变量执行物理级联删除）；**法律/隐私/安全要求处置 Personal Data 时必须进入受治理 Exceptional Erasure/Redaction Path，不得被普通 Soft Delete 代替**（但正常生命周期不删不得解释为无论法律要求如何永久保留全部个人数据）。**⑥ Immutable Business Versions**：正常生命周期继续 NO UPDATE / NO OVERWRITE / NO ROUTINE PHYSICAL DELETE（Invalidation/Rejection/Supersession/Restore 不删除历史版本；受删除要求影响的历史版本经 DQ-17 后续决定的受治理机制处理——Identity 与 Business Fact 分离 / 删除独立 Identity Mapping / 受治理 Redaction / 不可逆 Anonymization / 最小 Tombstone / 受影响引用状态；**DQ-15 不决定具体 Redaction/Anonymization 算法**）。**⑦ Audit 与 State Transition**：正常生命周期 Append-only（不得普通统一 TTL、不得 UPDATE 覆盖、更正经 Correction/Superseding/Reversal Record）；Audit Payload 最小化敏感数据（不因 Append-only 永久复制 Secret / Access Token / 完整 Provider Payload / 不必要 PII / 完整 Source Content）；Exceptional Redaction 与 Legal Hold 冲突由 DQ-17 和适用法律决定。**⑧ Source/Evidence/ContentObject**：仍被 Current / 必须保留的 Historical Business Version / EvidenceLink / Audit Requirement / Legal Hold / Security Incident / Dispute / Active Review / Regulatory Hold 引用的 Source/Evidence 数据不得删除；Raw Content / Normalized Artifact / Parsed Artifact / Fragment / EvidenceLink / Retrieval Index 独立生命周期；**Source Invalidation ≠ Source Blob 物理删除；Evidence Retraction ≠ 删除 EvidenceLink 历史**；EvidenceLink 不在底层 Source 删除后静默改绑另一 SourceVersion；删除 Source Content 依策略保留最小 Provenance/Hash/Tombstone/Deletion Proof；底层 Evidence 被删除或不可用时上层 Business Version 进入明确 Review / Invalidation / Evidence-unavailable 状态而非静默改变历史。**⑨ Content-addressed Object**：ContentObject 仅当 `active_reference_count = 0` / 无 legal-security-incident-regulatory hold / 无 pending transaction / Grace Period 完成 / Reconciliation 通过才可 Physical Purge；Hash Deduplication 不合并业务所有权/权限/Tenant/Retention Policy；单一主体或 Tenant 删除请求不删除其他仍合法需要的共享引用；**跨 Tenant/跨 Security Domain 误删必须阻止**；Object Purge 前重新核验正式数据库引用（不只依赖缓存 Reference Count）；删除后记录 Object Store 结果但证明中不复制被删除内容。**⑩ DerivedArtifact/Fragment/Retrieval Index**：DerivedArtifact/Fragment 可删除性考虑 EvidenceLink 与 Historical Business Version 引用；Retrieval Index 派生/可重建/非权威（通常可比 SourceVersion 更早清理；删除 Index 不删除权威 Source/Fragment/EvidenceLink；**删除 Source 后不得经 Index Rebuild 恢复依法已删除内容**；Vector / Embedding Metadata / Search Cache / Index Snapshot 进入删除范围；EvidenceLink 不依赖只有 Retrieval Index 才能解析的身份）。**⑪ Workflow Checkpoint**：继续 **DQ-13 Whole-thread Lifecycle Deletion**（仅 Workflow Terminal / 无有效 Lease / 无 Pending Resume / 无 Human Interrupt / 无 Retry Window / 无 Incident-Legal-Security Hold / Registry 标记可清理的 Thread 可删；ACTIVE / IN_PROGRESS / INTERRUPTED / AWAITING_REVIEW / FAILED_RETRYABLE / RECONCILING 不删；Checkpoint 删除不删除 Workflow 最小终态 / Business Result / Audit / Idempotency / Durable Work Intent / Provider Call Ledger / Domain Version / Evidence / Integration Event；默认 Whole-thread Delete、未验证 Partial Pruning 不采用；Registry 可保留最小 Terminal Summary）。**⑫ Durable Work Intent**：Pending / Claimed / Running / Retryable / Lease Active / Awaiting Manual Review 状态不删（仅 Terminal / 不可 Retry / 无有效 Lease / 无 Pending Claim / 无 Dead-letter Review / Business Result 已确认 / 所需 Audit 已保存 / Retention Horizon 已关闭后归档或清理；删除不删除对应业务结果）。**⑬ Integration Event Outbox**：仅所有规定 Destination 完成 / Delivery 状态确认 / Replay Window 关闭 / Incident Hold 解除 / Consumer-Destination Policy 允许后清理（不因单次 Publish 成功立即删除；Relay Crash / Destination Retry / Late Replay 风险纳入 Retention；Dead-letter / Manual Review 状态不删）。**⑭ Consumer Dedup / Idempotency**：承担正确性责任，保留覆盖 Maximum Legitimate Retry Window / Maximum Replay Window / Provider Ambiguity Window / Late Duplicate Delivery / Incident Investigation Window（重复消息或 Command 仍可能合法到达时不提前删除；删除不重新开放重复业务效果风险；具体时长留未来 Policy Table）。**⑮ Provider Payload / Model Output**：区分 Business Result / Provider Call Ledger / Raw Provider Request / Raw Provider Response / Model Prompt / Model Response / Tool Payload / Debug Trace（Business Result 按业务策略、Provider Call Ledger 按幂等/争议/诊断要求保留；**Raw Prompt / Raw Response / Tool Payload / Debug Trace 不默认永久保存**；保存完整 Raw Payload 须有明确目的和安全分类；不以「以后可能有用」无限期保留敏感原始载荷；Raw Payload 删除不破坏正式 Business Result 或必要 Provider Identity）。**⑯ Observability**：Logs / Metrics / Traces 属 RFC-007 非权威（可 Sampling / Aggregation / Redaction / Lower Precision / Archive / Purge；**Observability Retention 不替代 Audit Retention**；Audit 删除不依赖日志副本仍存在；日志不保存本应由 Retention Policy 删除的完整敏感 Payload）。**⑰ Migration / Backfill Records**：Alembic Revision History 正常保留；Migration Outcome / Verification Result / Forward Repair Evidence / Destructive Gate Approval 不作普通运行日志随意删除；临时 Backfill Batch / Lock Diagnostic / 低价值 Runtime Logs 可按策略清理（不破坏 Migration 可验证性或 Incident Investigation）。**⑱ Orphan Blob**：Upload 成功但 DB Commit 失败的 Blob 为 Orphan Candidate（删除前等待 Grace Period / 再次检查正式引用 / 排除 Crash Recovery / 排除 Eventual Visibility 风险 / 排除 Hold / 记录 Reconciliation Result；不在上传后立即删除未找到引用的对象；Orphan Cleanup 幂等）。**⑲ Policy Ownership**：**Central Retention Governance + Decentralized Data Ownership**（Governance 负责 Policy Schema / Policy Version / Hold Priority / Deletion Case / Execution Protocol / Reporting / Compliance Mapping；各模块仍是自身数据唯一所有者，负责判断 Eligibility / 提供 Reference-Dependency Check / 执行本模块处置 / 验证处置结果 / 返回最小 Deletion Proof；**中央 Purge Orchestrator 不得直接跨模块执行任意 SQL DELETE，必须经类型化 Application Contract 调用各所有模块**；DQ-02 唯一表所有权继续有效）。**⑳ Policy Versioning**：每项 Retention/Deletion 决定记录 `retention_policy_id` / `retention_policy_version` / Data Class / Policy Owner / Retention Trigger / Trigger Time / Computed Eligible Time / Disposition / Business Purpose / Legal-Contractual Basis / Hold Status / Decision Reason / Verification Result（Policy 修改不静默改写历史删除决定依据；Policy Version 可审计；Policy 更新后对存量数据批量 Purge 前重新影响分析和授权；新 Policy 不自动扩大旧 Deletion Case Scope）。**㉑ Hold**：至少支持 LEGAL_HOLD / SECURITY_INCIDENT_HOLD / REGULATORY_HOLD / DISPUTE_OR_CLAIM_HOLD / BUSINESS_REVIEW_HOLD（记录 Scope / Reason / Authority / Created Time / Review Time / Release Authority / Status / Audit Trail；**Hold 存在时可继续计算 Retention Clock 但必须阻止 Physical Purge**；Hold 不被普通 Retention Job 自动解除；不创建无 Owner / Reason / Review / Release 机制的永久 Hold；Hold Release 可审计）。**㉒ Deletion / Erasure Case**：**删除请求不得直接映射为单次 SQL DELETE**，每个请求形成受治理 Deletion/Erasure Case（Receive Request → Verify Requester/Authority → Determine Applicable Policy → Discover Data Scope → Check References and Holds → Build Deletion Plan → Execute by Owning Modules → Verify All Storage Planes → Record Completion or Residual State；记录为何执行/阻止/暂缓/继续保留；请求身份与权限验证；不自动包含其他主体或 Tenant 数据；Scope 扩展重新授权）。**㉓ Reference-aware Eligibility**：删除资格考虑完整依赖关系（ContentObject ← SourceVersion ← Fragment ← EvidenceLink ← BusinessVersion；仍承担正式业务含义的下游引用阻止底层对象直接删除；处置包括 Block Purge / Redact Allowed Fields / Retain Tombstone / Apply Governed Anonymization / Mark Evidence Unavailable / Trigger Business Review / Trigger Invalidation）；**未经审查的广泛跨模块 `ON DELETE CASCADE` 禁止**（Cascade 仅限同一所有模块内、生命周期完全一致、非权威或明确从属的 Child Record；Cascade 行为在未来 Data Retention Policy Table 明确）。**㉔ Deletion State Machine**：Deletion Case 至少区分 REQUESTED / ASSESSING / BLOCKED_BY_REFERENCE / HELD / ELIGIBLE / PURGE_IN_PROGRESS / PRIMARY_PURGED / DERIVED_PURGED / OBJECT_PURGED / BACKUP_EXPIRY_PENDING / COMPLETED / FAILED_RETRYABLE / FAILED_MANUAL_REVIEW（**COMPLETED 不得在只删除一张表后立即设置；PRIMARY_PURGED 不表示 Object/Index/Cache/Backup 已完成处置；BACKUP_EXPIRY_PENDING 为合法中间状态**；Failed 状态保留可恢复信息）。**㉕ Purge Orchestration**：Purge Worker 遵循 **DQ-07 Lease/Attempt/Fencing Token 与 DQ-08 Idempotency**，分批短事务，支持 Crash Recovery / Resume / Safe Retry / Storage-plane-specific Progress / Manual Review / Partial Failure Classification；**重复执行同一 Deletion Case 不得扩大删除范围 / 删除新创建的合法引用 / 删除其他 Tenant 或主体数据 / 生成互相矛盾的 Tombstone / 重复触发不可逆外部副作用**；每个 Storage Plane 独立状态和验证结果；Final Completion 在全部要求 Storage Plane 完成或明确记录 Backup Expiry Pending 后确定。**㉖ PostgreSQL 删除边界**：区分 Application Inaccessibility / Logical Database Deletion / Physical Storage Reclamation / Backup Expiry（**SQL `DELETE` Commit 表示事务中逻辑删除完成，不得描述为底层所有字节立即消失**；VACUUM / 存储重用 / 物理空间回收属数据库存储生命周期；Physical Reclamation ≠ Backup Expiry；不为证明删除完成执行未经风险评估的 `VACUUM FULL` 或高风险操作）。**㉗ Backup / PITR**：Primary Store 删除后数据可能仍存在于受控 Backup/PITR Window（删除状态表达 PRIMARY_PURGED / BACKUP_EXPIRY_PENDING / BACKUP_RETENTION_COMPLETED；**从旧 Backup 恢复必须隔离恢复环境 / 重放已完成 Deletion Ledger / 重新应用 Hold-Erasure 状态 / 验证已删除数据未重新进入服务 / 完成验证后再开放流量——Database Restore 不得使已完成删除的数据静默复活**；Backup/PITR 具体周期与基础设施方案本次不决定；Backup Cleanup 不由普通业务 Purge Worker 直接操作）。**㉘ Tombstone / Deletion Proof**：删除后可保留最小 Tombstone/Deletion Proof（可含 Deletion Case ID / Subject Scope Hash / Data Class / Policy Version / Disposition / Completed Time / Storage Planes Completed / Hold-Exception Reference / Executor Identity；**不含完整原始 Payload / 被要求删除的完整 PII / Secret / 完整 Provider Response / 完整 Source Content**；足以证明执行范围和结果但不重新构成被删除数据副本；Archive / Cold Storage / Encryption / Soft Delete / Access Restriction 均不得声称为 Physical Purge）。**㉙ Readiness Artifact**：任何正式 Retention 或 Physical Purge 实现授权前 Architecture Readiness Package 必须包含 **Data Retention, Hold & Deletion Policy Table**（至少 Data Class / Owning Module / Authoritative-Derived / Contains PII-Secret / Business Purpose / Legal-Contractual Basis / Retention Trigger / Retention Clock / Minimum Required Horizon / Maximum Permitted Horizon / Normal Disposition / Exceptional Erasure Action / Reference Blockers / Hold Types / Archive Target / Primary Store Deletion / Object Store Deletion / Index-Cache Deletion / Checkpoint Deletion / Backup-PITR Treatment / Tombstone-Deletion Proof / Verification Owner / Related DQ-DEC-RFC）——**DQ-15 接受不授权创建该表、不授权填写任何具体保留期限、不新增独立 Matrix**。**㉚ Technical Spike**：首个生产 Purge 实现前必须完成单独授权的 **Retention & Deletion Safety Technical Spike**（最低验证 Reference-aware Eligibility / Legal Hold 阻止删除 / Security-Incident Hold 阻止删除 / Idempotent Purge Retry / Purge Worker Lease-Fencing / Checkpoint Whole-thread Delete / Pending Workflow 不被删除 / 多引用 ContentObject 保护 / Orphan Blob Grace Period / Retrieval Index-Cache Cleanup / Provider Payload Cleanup / SQL Delete 与 Physical Reclamation 边界 / Backup Restore 后重放 Deletion Ledger / Primary-Object-Index 删除步骤之间的 Crash / Deletion Proof 不含原敏感载荷 / 不发生跨 Tenant-跨主体-跨安全域误删；使用项目实际选定 PostgreSQL / Object Storage / Checkpoint / Backup 测试环境；可共享测试基础设施但独立验收报告）——**REQUIRED BEFORE PRODUCTION PURGE IMPLEMENTATION 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**。**㉛ 授权边界**：DQ-15 只授权决策归档与文档同步，不授权创建 Retention Policy Registry / Data Retention Policy Table / Deletion-Erasure Case / Hold Registry / Purge Worker / Deletion Ledger / Tombstone / Retention Job / Object Lifecycle Rule / Checkpoint Cleanup Job / Backup Cleanup / Database DDL / Migration / Tests / Infrastructure，不授权设置具体保留期限，不授权执行任何数据删除，不授权接受 DQ-16 或 DQ-17；精确 PII 分离 / Redaction / Anonymization / Encryption / 法律例外机制留 DQ-17。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：分类定责，业务真值/审计 append-only 不删，checkpoint/运行记录由应用层可回收，原始来源保留策略留合规决定，具体周期数值由用户定不虚构（[架构推断]，置信度中）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + Formal Model 八要素 + Universal TTL / Universal Permanent Retention 拒绝 + 生命周期术语分离 + Retention Policy 分类 + Retention Clock + Business Current Truth + Immutable Versions + Audit + Source/Evidence/ContentObject + Content-addressed Object + DerivedArtifact/Index + Checkpoint + Work Intent + Outbox + Dedup/Idempotency + Provider Payload + Observability + Migration Records + Orphan Blob + Policy Ownership + Policy Versioning + Hold + Deletion/Erasure Case + Reference-aware Eligibility + Cascade 边界 + Deletion State Machine + Purge Orchestration + PostgreSQL 删除边界 + Backup/PITR + Tombstone/Deletion Proof + Readiness Table/Spike 前置；Candidate B Universal TTL 拒绝；Candidate C Universal Permanent Retention 拒绝）。
14. **持久化测试策略 = Layered Test Strategy（DQ-16 Accepted Decision，2026-08-03，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A；Accepted Principle = LAYERED TEST STRATEGY）**：**① Formal Model 与两类拒绝**：MVP 采用分层测试模型 **Pure Domain/Application Unit Tests + Port Contract Parity Tests + Real PostgreSQL Persistence Acceptance Tests + Deterministic Multi-connection Concurrency Tests + Real Migration/Upgrade/Recovery Tests + Crash-window/Fault-injection Tests + Production-topology-specific Qualification**；**Candidate B（全部真实 PostgreSQL 通用策略）拒绝——REJECTED AS A UNIVERSAL ALL-TESTS-USE-POSTGRESQL POLICY**（并非所有测试必须使用 PostgreSQL：Pure Domain Unit Test 不使用数据库）；**Candidate C（全部 SQLite fake）拒绝——ALL-SQLITE TESTING CANNOT PROVE POSTGRESQL SEMANTICS**。**② Pure Domain 与 Application 测试边界**：Pure Domain Unit Test 不使用数据库；Application Use-case Test 可以使用 In-memory Fake / Stub / Spy / Deterministic Clock / Deterministic ID Generator；**Application Test Double 只能证明 Application Contract，不得证明 SQL Constraint / Transaction Atomicity / Commit Visibility / PostgreSQL Lock / MVCC / CAS / Migration / Idempotency / Crash Recovery**。**③ SQLite 边界**：**SQLite 仅为可选、非权威开发 Test Double，不得作为 Persistence Contract / Persistence Acceptance Engine / Concurrency Proof / Transaction Proof / Migration Proof / Idempotency Proof / Release Readiness Evidence**。**④ 真实 PostgreSQL 验收范围**：Repository / UnitOfWork / ORM Mapping / PostgreSQL Types / Constraint / Transaction / Commit-Rollback / Concurrency / CAS / Migration / Idempotency / Lease-Fencing / Durable Work Intent / Outbox / Audit / Domain Version / Current Truth Pointer / Retention Referential Safety 正式测试必须使用真实 PostgreSQL；**SQLite Test 通过不得被描述为 PostgreSQL 语义通过**。**⑤ Port Contract Parity**：每个 Persistence Port 应具有可复用 Contract Suite；同一 Contract Suite 可以运行于 In-memory Test Double 和 PostgreSQL Adapter，但 **Fake Pass = Test Double 符合 Application 可观察契约，PostgreSQL Adapter Pass = 正式 Persistence Adapter 符合持久化契约，Fake 通过不得替代 PostgreSQL Adapter 通过**；每个 Test Double 必须明确声明 Supported Contract / Unsupported Semantics / Transaction Model / Concurrency Model / Error Model / Time Model / ID Model / Cleanup Strategy / 与 PostgreSQL 的差异。**⑥ MVP Persistence Stack**：MVP 只测试已接受的正式持久化 Stack（**SQLAlchemy 2.x Synchronous API + Psycopg 3 Synchronous Driver**）；**Async SQLAlchemy、Async Psycopg 或 Asyncpg 不属于 MVP Persistence Acceptance Scope**；未来引入 Async Stack 必须作为新的架构和兼容性决定处理。**⑦ 测试环境与证据元数据**：正式测试环境必须使用项目钉定版本的 PostgreSQL / SQLAlchemy / Psycopg / Alembic；必须使用独立 Test Role、隔离 Database 或 Schema，不连接 Shared Development Database、不连接 Production Database、不使用生产数据、不使用真实 Secret，应用正式 Alembic Migration、使用正式默认 Isolation Level；测试证据必须记录 PostgreSQL Version / SQLAlchemy Version / Psycopg Version / Alembic Head / Application Commit SHA / Isolation Level / Worker Count / Connection Count / Pool-Pooler Mode / Test Seed。**⑧ 事务 Fixture 边界**：单连接且不验证真实 Commit Visibility 的 Adapter Test 可以使用 External Transaction + SAVEPOINT + Test-end Rollback；**Multi-connection / Commit Visibility / Outbox / Consumer / Connection Pool / Deadlock / Serialization Retry / Worker Crash / Migration / Multi-worker Claim / Checkpoint / 跨存储 Crash Window 测试必须执行真实 Commit**；多连接测试不得被一个永不提交的外层事务包裹；并行 CI Worker 必须拥有独立 Database、Schema、Namespace 或等价隔离。**⑨ 确定性并发测试**：并发 Actor 必须使用独立 Connection / Session / Transaction；**不得在多个 Actor 间共享同一 SQLAlchemy Session**；并发测试必须使用 Barrier / Latch / Event / 明确 Blocking Point / 可观测 Transaction State / 受控 Worker Coordination，**不得主要依赖 `sleep`**；必须验证成功 Actor / 冲突 Actor / 项目错误 / 真实 SQLSTATE / Retry Count / Final Business Current Truth / 无部分写入 / 无孤立 Domain Version / 无重复 Work Intent / 无重复业务效果；Deadlock Test 不得固定断言特定 Worker 必须被终止（应验证一个事务被中止且最终业务不变量成立）；必须真实覆盖——相同 `expected_revision` 竞争、CAS 一个成功一个冲突、Version Number 并发分配、命名唯一约束竞争、SQLSTATE `40001`、SQLSTATE `40P01`、最多三次 Transaction 总尝试、Retry 复用同一 Command Identity、Retry 不产生额外 Domain Version、Work Intent 并发 Claim、`SKIP LOCKED` 不重复 Claim、Lease Expiry 与 Takeover、Stale Fencing Token 无法提交、相同 Idempotency Key + 相同 Fingerprint、相同 Key + 不同 Fingerprint、Outbox/Consumer Duplicate Delivery、Promotion/Invalidation 竞争、Restore/New Write 竞争、Purge/New Reference 竞争。**⑩ Atomic Commit Fault Injection**：每个 DEC-035 Atomic Business Commit 必须通过 Fault Injection 验证全有或全无，至少在 First Write 前 / Domain Version Insert 后 / Current Truth Pointer Update 后 / Evidence Link 后 / Audit 后 / Idempotency Result 后 / Integration Event Outbox 后 / Durable Work Intent 后 / Commit 前 / Commit-Connection Outcome 不确定时注入失败；必须验证不存在业务成功但 Audit 缺失 / 业务失败但 Work Intent 可领取 / 事务失败但 Outbox 可发布 / CAS 冲突后部分记录保留 / Retry 产生重复正式版本；**Commit Outcome Unknown 不得只通过 Mock `session.commit()` 抛异常验证**；Crash/Failure Test 应覆盖真实或语义等价的 Connection Termination / Process Kill / Timeout / Deadlock / Serialization Failure / Partial External Success。**⑪ Crash Window 覆盖**：Business Commit 成功+Checkpoint 写入失败、Checkpoint 成功+Business Commit 失败、Blob Upload 成功+Database Finalization 失败、Database 引用未验证 Blob、Provider Side Effect 成功+本地记录失败、Work Intent Claim 后 Worker Crash、Outbox Publish 后状态更新前 Crash、Primary Purge 成功+Object-Index 删除前 Crash、Backup Restore 后 Deletion Ledger 未重放。**⑫ 幂等覆盖**：每个 Idempotent Use Case 至少覆盖 First Execution / Exact Replay / Concurrent Exact Replay / Same Key Different Fingerprint / Transient Failure Retry / Permanent Failure / Commit Outcome Unknown / Intentional Rerun / Retention Window Closed；必须验证 Replay 返回同一正式结果 / Provider 不被重复调用 / 不产生第二个 Business Version / 不重复写 Audit / 不重复创建 Work Intent / Fingerprint Conflict 明确失败 / Intentional Rerun 使用新身份 / Retry 与 Rerun 保持不同语义。**⑬ 迁移覆盖**：Migration Test 必须使用真实 PostgreSQL，至少覆盖 Fresh Database→Head / Supported Release Baseline→Head / Single Head / Multiple Head Detection / Drift Detection / Vendor Schema Exclusion / Old Application + Expanded Schema / New Application + Expanded Schema / Application Rollback + Expanded Schema / Backfill Pause-Resume / Backfill Idempotency / `NOT VALID`-`VALIDATE` / Concurrent Index Failure / Invalid Index Cleanup / Forward Repair / Destructive Gate / Contract 在旧实例存在时被阻止；**`metadata.create_all()` 不得作为 Migration Acceptance 的唯一 Schema 来源**。**⑭ PR Required 与 Scheduled Tier**：Required PR Checks 必须包含 correctness-critical 的 PostgreSQL Transaction / Constraint / Concurrency / Idempotency / Migration；高强度 Contention / 长时间 Recovery / Live Provider / Backup Restore / Performance / 长周期 Soak Test 可以进入 Scheduled/Manual Tier；**Correctness-critical Invariant 不得只在 Scheduled/Nightly Test 中验证**。**⑮ Flaky Test 政策**：**禁止通过自动重跑将 Flaky Persistence Test 变绿**；必须区分 Application Retry Under Test ≠ CI Test Retry；基础设施启动失败可以有限重试，但业务断言失败 / 并发结果不稳定 / Timeout / SQLSTATE 异常 / 最终行状态错误不得被自动 Retry 掩盖；Flaky Required Check 应视为测试或架构缺陷；并发失败报告必须包含 Seed / Actor Timeline / Transaction Identity / SQLSTATE / Retry Count / Final Rows / Lock-Timeout Diagnostic。**⑯ 合成/敏感测试数据**：测试数据必须使用合成数据、不含生产 PII / 真实 Credential / 真实 Provider Token、不复制真实 Checkpoint / 真实 Prompt-Provider Payload、使用受控 Secret Injection、失败日志执行 Redaction；精确 Sensitive Test Fixture 规则继续由 DQ-17 决定。**⑰ Readiness Artifact**：Architecture Readiness Package 必须包含 **Persistence Test Coverage & Fidelity Table**（至少 Requirement-Invariant / Owning DQ-DEC-RFC / Test Layer / Test Subject / Real PostgreSQL Required / Connection Count / Process-Worker Count / Isolation Level / Fixture Strategy / Commit Visibility Required / Expected SQLSTATE / Fault Injection / External Dependency / Cleanup Strategy / CI Tier / Required-Optional / Evidence Produced / Owner，18 项字段）——**DQ-16 接受不授权创建该表**。**⑱ Technical Spike 与 Common Harness**：**DQ-16 不新增独立通用 Technical Spike**；已有专项 Spike（PostgreSQL Concurrency / External Object Consistency / Workflow Checkpoint Isolation / Schema Migration Rollout / Retention & Deletion Safety）继续有效，且必须共享合格的真实 PostgreSQL Test Harness 原则（真实 PostgreSQL / 钉定版本 / 确定性协调 / 环境记录 / 可复核证据 / 不使用 SQLite 作为验收引擎）；**Common Persistence Test Harness Qualification 必须作为第一个获授权 Persistence Spike 的组成部分（NOT AUTHORIZED）**；本次接受不授权创建 Harness、Container、CI Job、Fixture、Contract Suite、测试代码或基础设施。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：快速 fake 跑单元/契约、真实目标引擎跑并发/事务/迁移/幂等语义（[架构推断]，置信度中-高）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + 七层 Formal Model + Test Double 边界 + SQLite 非权威边界 + 真实 PostgreSQL 验收清单 + Port Contract Parity + Sync-only MVP Stack + 测试环境与证据元数据 + 事务 Fixture 边界 + 确定性并发 + Fault Injection + Crash Window + 幂等覆盖 + 迁移覆盖 + PR/Scheduled Tier + Flaky 政策 + 合成数据 + Coverage & Fidelity Table + Spike/Harness 关系；Candidate B Universal All-Tests-Use-PostgreSQL 拒绝；Candidate C All-SQLite Testing 拒绝）。
15. **安全与敏感数据边界 = Classified Sensitive-data Protection + Secret-reference-only Persistence + Ephemeral Adapter-scoped Secret Resolution + Data-minimized Multi-plane Propagation + Strict Allowlisted Checkpoint Serialization + Least-privilege Role/Credential/Pool Separation + Selective Envelope Encryption by Protection Profile + Infrastructure Encryption in Transit/at Rest + Auditable Access/Redaction/Rotation/Incident Response（DQ-17 Accepted Decision，2026-08-03，ACCEPTED WITH MAJOR REVISION；Accepted Candidate = Candidate A）**：**① Formal Model 与候选处置**：MVP 采用上述正式安全模型；**Candidate B = NOT SELECTED AS UNIVERSAL FIELD ENCRYPTION**（应用层字段级加密不作所有字段通用策略；SELECTIVE AUTHENTICATED ENVELOPE ENCRYPTION = CONDITIONAL ON DATA PROTECTION PROFILE）；**Candidate C = REJECTED AS SOLE SECURITY CONTROL**（依赖 DB 静态加密不作唯一安全控制；Infrastructure At-rest Encryption 保留为 REQUIRED INFRASTRUCTURE DEFENSE IN DEPTH）。**② Secret 与业务数据分类**：SECRET ≠ PII ≠ SENSITIVE BUSINESS DATA ≠ PUBLIC/INTERNAL BUSINESS DATA 语义独立；API Key/Access Token/Refresh Token/Database Password/Private Key/Webhook Signing Secret/Encryption Key/Provider Credential/其他能够授予系统访问能力的认证或加密材料属于 Secret 而非可持久化业务数据；**Secret Value 不得进入 Domain/Application Command/Business Current Truth/Business Version/Graph State/Checkpoint/Workflow Execution Registry/Audit/State Transition/Domain-Application-Integration Event/Outbox/Durable Work Intent/Idempotency Record/Idempotency Fingerprint/Source/SourceVersion/Evidence/Retrieval Index/Cache/Log/Trace/Metric/Error/Test Snapshot/Object Key 或其他持久化或派生存储**。**③ Secret Reference 与解析边界**：允许持久化的只能是无明文能力的引用（credential_ref/secret_reference_id/provider_account_id/secret_version_reference/credential_profile_id）；正式执行流程 = Application selects Credential Reference → Infrastructure Adapter resolves Secret ephemerally → Adapter performs one authorized external interaction → Adapter returns sanitized business result → Secret Value is not returned to Application/Workflow/Domain；Application/Domain/Workflow 可以知道使用哪个 Credential Profile，但不得获取 Secret Value；具体 Secret Manager/KMS/Vault/HSM/部署注入产品不由 RFC-002 决定。**④ 数据分类模型**：敏感业务数据采用 CONFIDENTIALITY LEVEL + HANDLING TAGS（Level 至少 PUBLIC/INTERNAL/CONFIDENTIAL/RESTRICTED；Tags 可含 PII/PROVIDER_PAYLOAD/MODEL_CONTENT/USER_CONTENT/LEGAL_HOLD/SECURITY_INCIDENT/EXPORT_RESTRICTED/AUTH_CREDENTIAL）；`SECRET` 是具有独立禁止持久化规则的特殊类别，不能只当作普通 `RESTRICTED` 字段处理；每个字段或 Payload 必须明确 Purpose/Data Owner/Confidentiality Level/Handling Tags/Authoritative Store/Derived Stores/Plaintext Allowed Path/Authorized Roles/Redaction/Transport Encryption/At-rest Encryption/Field-level Encryption Requirement/Retention/Erasure/Test Fixture Rule/Incident Response。**⑤ 多持久化平面传播**：数据分类和处理规则必须传播到所有适用平面（PostgreSQL/Object Storage/Checkpoint/Workflow Registry/Audit/State Transition/Durable Work Intent/Integration Event Outbox/Retrieval Index/Cache/Logs/Traces/Metrics/Backup-PITR/Test Fixture/CI Artifact/Dead-letter Record）；**不得只对 PostgreSQL Column 做分类却让完整敏感 Payload 无限制进入派生存储**。**⑥ Secret Wrapper 边界**：`SecretStr`/Masked String/隐藏 repr() 或类似 Wrapper 只能降低偶然显示风险（MASKED REPRESENTATION ≠ NON-PERSISTENCE GUARANTEE）；不得依赖 Wrapper 阻止调用 get_secret_value()/转换成普通字符串/放入 Dictionary/放入 Graph State/被 Serializer 序列化/写入错误上下文/写入日志/写入 Checkpoint；**Secret Non-persistence 必须由类型边界、架构测试、Serializer Allowlist、Sink Redaction 与禁止路径共同强制**。**⑦ Checkpoint 严格序列化**：LANGGRAPH_STRICT_MSGPACK = REQUIRED；Explicit allowed_msgpack_modules Allowlist = REQUIRED OR PREFERRED ACCORDING TO PINNED VERSION；**Pickle Fallback = PROHIBITED**；不得允许任意 Python Module/任意未登记 Class/任意 Pydantic Model/任意 Dataclass/Pickle Fallback/未审查自定义对象/来自不可信 Checkpoint Storage 的任意类型反序列化；Graph State 优先 String/Integer/Boolean/List/Dict/Opaque ID/Versioned Typed Primitive Payload；恶意或损坏的 Checkpoint 必须进入 INCOMPATIBLE/CORRUPT/SECURITY_REJECTED 或等价受控失败分类，不得尝试不安全降级反序列化。**⑧ Checkpoint 加密边界**：Secret Value 无论是否启用 Checkpoint Encryption 都不得进入 Graph State 或 Checkpoint（**ENCRYPTED CHECKPOINT ≠ PERMISSION TO STORE SECRETS**）；经分类允许持久化的敏感 Runtime State 可采用经验证的 EncryptedSerializer 或等价加密序列化机制；不得假设 Thread ID/Checkpoint ID/Namespace/Metadata/调度字段/查询索引/Vendor Migration Metadata/Connection Metadata 全部自动加密；实际加密范围必须基于项目钉定的 LangGraph/PostgresSaver 版本/实际 Serializer/实际 PostgreSQL Schema/实际部署 Pool-Pooler/实际查询路径进行验证；Encrypted Checkpoint 仍必须遵守最小化/Least Privilege/Retention/Whole-thread Deletion/Incident Response/Access Audit。**⑨ 三层加密责任**：Transport Encryption（PostgreSQL/Object Storage/Secret Provider/KMS/外部 Provider 和其他网络连接必须使用经过验证的加密传输；具体 TLS/CA/证书和部署配置由后续 Infrastructure/Deployment 决策负责）；Infrastructure At-rest Encryption（PostgreSQL Volume/Database Backup-PITR/Object Storage/Checkpoint Storage/Log-Trace Storage/可能包含敏感数据的 CI Artifact/其他持久化平面必须具备基础设施级静态加密；**INFRASTRUCTURE ENCRYPTION AT REST = REQUIRED BASELINE 且 NOT SUFFICIENT AS SOLE APPLICATION CONTROL**，不替代 Application Authorization/Data Minimization/Redaction/Least Privilege/Retention/Field-level Protection）；Selective Application-level Envelope Encryption（**UNIVERSAL FIELD-LEVEL ENCRYPTION = REJECTED；SELECTIVE AUTHENTICATED ENVELOPE ENCRYPTION = CONDITIONAL ON DATA PROTECTION PROFILE**；威胁模型包括 Database Read-only Credential 泄漏/Backup 泄漏/运维人员不应读取明文/Tenant-Security Domain 需要独立 Key/独立撤销/Crypto-shredding/法律合同业务保护要求）。**⑩ Ciphertext 与 Key 分离**：采用 Envelope Encryption 时数据库可以保存 Ciphertext/Algorithm ID/Key Reference/Key Version/Nonce-IV/Authentication Tag/Encrypted DEK（如适用），**不得保存明文 DEK/明文 KEK/Master Encryption Key/Secret Manager Root Credential**；密钥生命周期至少表达 CREATE/ACTIVATE/ROTATE/RETIRE/REVOKE/DESTROY/COMPROMISE RESPONSE；必须区分新写入使用新 Key/旧数据仍可读取/渐进式 Re-encryption/紧急 Compromise Rotation/Backup 中旧 Key 的恢复需求/Key Destruction 对历史数据可恢复性的影响；本次不选择 KMS/Vault/HSM/算法/Rotation Period/Key Hierarchy 实现。**⑪ 禁止未经治理的加密实现**：不得使用未经治理的 pgcrypto raw encrypt-decrypt/custom AES helper/home-grown crypto/key and ciphertext in the same database；字段级加密如获授权必须采用 AUTHENTICATED ENCRYPTION + EXTERNAL KEY MANAGEMENT + ALGORITHM/KEY VERSION METADATA + ROTATION AND RE-ENCRYPTION PLAN；本次不授权创建任何 Encryption Adapter/Encrypted Column/Key Registry/Re-encryption Job。**⑫ PostgreSQL Least Privilege**：必须区分 Migration Owner Role/Business Runtime Write Role/Business Read-only Role/Checkpoint Runtime Role/Dispatch-Worker Role/Purge Role/Test Role/Observability Role；**Runtime Role 不得是 Superuser、拥有 BYPASSRLS/CREATEROLE/CREATEDB、是 Business Table Owner、拥有任意 Business Schema DDL、修改 Alembic Version、修改其他 Persistence Plane、读取未授权敏感列、访问不属于其能力范围的 Schema**；Business/Checkpoint/Migration/Purge/Test/Observability Connection-Pool 必须保持分离；Migration Credential 不得进入 Web-Worker-Checkpoint-Test Runtime/Developer Local Config/普通 Application Environment；应撤销不需要的 PUBLIC 权限、保护 public Schema、采用固定受控 search_path（具体 Role/Grant/Schema 本次不授权创建）。**⑬ Row-level Security**：RLS 可作 Multi-tenant 纵深防御但不替代 DQ-02 Module Ownership/Application Authorization/Public Application Contract/Business Invariant/Repository Boundary（**RLS = OPTIONAL DEFENSE IN DEPTH，NOT REQUIRED FOR EVERY TABLE**）；未来启用必须验证 Runtime Role 非 Table Owner/无 BYPASSRLS/Tenant Context 来源明确/Pool 复用不泄漏 Tenant Context/Worker 不绕过 Policy/Purge-Migration-Incident Role 独立策略/使用真实 PostgreSQL 测试；本次不授权创建任何 RLS Policy。**⑭ Credential 与连接池分离**：BUSINESS POOL ≠ CHECKPOINT POOL ≠ MIGRATION CONNECTION ≠ PURGE CONNECTION ≠ TEST CONNECTION；不同能力不得共享万能数据库账号；Secret 应尽可能短生命周期/动态签发/最小 Scope/可撤销/自动轮换/独立审计；Purge Credential 只能用于受治理的 Deletion Case。**⑮ Redaction 顺序**：不得 Persist Full Payload → Attempt Cleanup Later；必须 **CLASSIFY → MINIMIZE → REDACT → SERIALIZE/EMIT/PERSIST**；Redaction 必须发生在进入 Audit/State Transition/Error/Log/Trace/Metric Label/Outbox/Durable Work Intent/Checkpoint/Retrieval Index/Cache/Dead-letter Record/Test Failure Snapshot/Provider Diagnostic/CI Artifact 之前；优先基于结构化字段和 Data Classification，不得只依赖全局 Regex。**⑯ 安全 Audit**：敏感数据读取/批量导出/Decryption/Secret Resolution/Credential Rotation/Key Rotation/权限修改/Break-glass Access/Deletion-Erasure/Hold 创建与解除/数据分类变更/反序列化拒绝/Checkpoint Integrity Failure/Security Exception 应生成安全 Audit Metadata（记录 actor/action/target identity/classification/purpose/result/occurred_at/correlation_id/authorization decision；**Audit 不得记录 Secret Value/明文 Key/完整敏感 Payload/完整 Decrypted Field/完整 Prompt/完整 Provider Response/完整 Source Content**；本次不授权创建 Security Audit 表或实现）。**⑰ Provider、Prompt 与 Model Output**：Provider Response/User Prompt/Model Output/Tool Result/Source Content/Provider Diagnostic 必须视为不可信且可能敏感（可能包含 PII/被回显 Secret/第三方数据/Prompt Injection/临时访问 URL/Token/Provider Internal Metadata；**MODEL-GENERATED ≠ SAFE TO PERSIST**）；进入 Business Version/Source-Evidence/Checkpoint/Audit/Retrieval Index/Log-Trace/Test Fixture 前必须重新分类、最小化和 Redact。**⑱ Object Key 与 Content Hash**：Object Key 不得包含 PII/Email/Source URL/Tenant Name/敏感原始 Filename/Credential/Token/Secret/可直接识别主体的信息；Content Hash 不得作为公开存在性查询接口/被视为匿名化证明/暴露给未授权调用方/用作 Secret Value 的普通 Idempotency Fingerprint（低熵敏感内容的 Hash 可能构成存在性或 Dictionary Oracle，必须限制访问；Blind Index/Deterministic Encryption/可搜索加密必须作为独立安全设计审查）。**⑲ 跨 Tenant 与跨安全域去重**：DQ-12 暂缓的边界在 DQ-17 正式关闭——**CROSS-TENANT CONTENT DEDUPLICATION = PROHIBITED FOR MVP；CROSS-SECURITY-DOMAIN CONTENT DEDUPLICATION = PROHIBITED FOR MVP**；同一 Tenant/同一 Security Domain 内允许的物理 ContentObject 去重仍必须满足相同 Data Classification/相同访问域/Retention 兼容/Legal Hold 兼容/不暴露内容是否存在/删除一个逻辑引用不删除其他合法引用/DQ-12 Reference Integrity/DQ-15 Reference-aware Purge。**⑳ 测试数据与 Secret Detection**：测试只能使用合成数据（禁止 Production Database Dump/真实用户 PII/真实 API Key/真实 Provider Token/Production Checkpoint/Production Prompt-Response/Production Encryption Key/Production Presigned URL/Production Credential/真实敏感 Source Content）；Test Credential 必须最小 Scope/只访问 Sandbox/不访问 Production/可自动失效/可立即撤销/不写入 Fixture Snapshot/不暴露给 Fork PR；**Secret Detection Failure 必须阻止 Merge**；发现 Secret 泄漏后必须先执行 REVOKE → ROTATE → INVESTIGATE HISTORY/LOG/ARTIFACT EXPOSURE → REMOVE OR REWRITE ONLY WHEN AUTHORIZED，不得仅从最新文件删除字符串后声称问题已经解决。**㉑ Retention 与 Erasure 边界**：DQ-15 继续拥有 Retention Period/Deletion Case/Hold/Purge/Backup Expiry；DQ-17 决定 Secret Value 不得因 Retention Policy 而长期持久化/Encrypted Data 仍属于受保护数据/Key Destruction 不得未经验证地等同于完整删除/Redacted-Aggregated Data 是否可重识别必须重新评估/Backup Restore 必须恢复适用 Key Version 与 Deletion Ledger/Legal Hold 不要求保留 Secret Value/Deletion Proof 不得保存被删除的敏感 Payload。**㉒ 所有权边界**：Security Governance 拥有 Classification Vocabulary/Handling Rules/Cryptographic Policy/Least-privilege Baseline/Security Exception/Incident Response/Security Review Gate；业务模块拥有自身字段分类/处理目的/最小化/Application Authorization/Redaction Contract/Retention Dependency/Sensitive Access Audit；Infrastructure 拥有 Secret Resolution Adapter/KMS-Vault Integration/TLS/Volume-Backup Encryption/PostgreSQL Role-Grant/Connection Credential/Encrypted Serializer Configuration/Credential-Key Rotation Execution；后续边界 RFC-003 = Graph State/Checkpoint Runtime Configuration、RFC-004 = API Authentication/Authorization/Transport、RFC-006 = LLM and Provider Secret Injection、RFC-007 = Logging/Trace Redaction/Security Monitoring、DQ-15 = Retention/Erasure/Backup Expiry。**㉓ Readiness Artifact**：Architecture Readiness Package 必须包含 **Sensitive Data, Secret & Cryptographic Control Matrix**（26 项字段：Data Element/Owning Module/Confidentiality Level/Handling Tags/Purpose/Secret or Business Data/Authoritative Store/Derived Stores/Plaintext Allowed/Graph State Allowed/Checkpoint Allowed/Audit Allowed/Outbox-Work Intent Allowed/Index-Cache Allowed/Object Store Allowed/Backup Allowed/Required Redaction/Transport Encryption/Infrastructure At-rest Encryption/Field-level Encryption/Key Owner-Reference/Authorized Roles/Retention-Erasure/Test Fixture Rule/Incident Response/Related DQ-DEC-RFC）——**REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED（本次接受不授权创建该 Matrix）**。**㉔ Security Qualification 与 Spike**：**DQ-17 不新增覆盖所有系统的独立通用 Technical Spike（New Independent DQ-17 Technical Spike = NOT REQUIRED）**；必须将 Cross-cutting Persistence Security Qualification 加入首个获授权的 PostgreSQL Concurrency Spike/External Object Consistency Spike/Workflow Checkpoint Isolation Spike/Schema Migration Rollout Spike/Retention Safety Spike/Common Persistence Test Harness Qualification（最低安全验证 17 项：Strict Msgpack Allowlist/Pickle Disabled/Malicious Checkpoint 不触发任意代码/Secret 不进入 Checkpoint-Audit-Log-Outbox-Work Intent/EncryptedSerializer 实际范围/PostgreSQL Role Denial/Runtime Role 非 Owner-Superuser-BYPASSRLS/Schema-Search Path 安全/Object Key 无敏感值/Cross-tenant-Security-domain Dedup 被拒绝/Redaction/Synthetic Fixture/Secret Scan/Backup-Key Version Recovery）——**REQUIRED IN FIRST AUTHORIZED PERSISTENCE SPIKES 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；未来选择 Application-level Field Encryption 必须单独授权 **Selective Field Encryption & Key Rotation Spike（CONDITIONALLY REQUIRED / NOT AUTHORIZED）**。历史 Recommendation「倾向 A：Secret 不落持久化真值/checkpoint/审计，业务敏感列分类 + least privilege，checkpoint 反序列化白名单，加密/密钥管理移交后续（[架构推断]，置信度高）」已被重大修订取代（Superseded by Accepted Major Revision：Candidate A 接受并修订 + 正式安全模型九要素 + Secret/PII/敏感业务数据语义独立 + Secret-reference-only 持久化 + ephemeral adapter-scoped 解析 + 数据分类模型（Level + Tags）+ 多平面传播 + Secret Wrapper 边界 + 严格 Msgpack + Checkpoint 加密边界 + 三层加密责任 + Ciphertext-Key 分离 + 禁止未经治理加密 + Least Privilege Role-Pool 分离 + RLS 边界 + Credential-连接池分离 + Redaction 顺序 + 安全 Audit + Provider-Prompt-Model Output 边界 + Object Key-Content Hash + 跨 Tenant-跨安全域去重禁止 + 测试数据与 Secret Detection + Retention-Erasure 边界 + 所有权边界 + Readiness Matrix + Security Qualification/Conditional Field Encryption Spike；Candidate B Universal Field Encryption 拒绝（条件启用）；Candidate C Sole Security Control 拒绝（保留为必备纵深防御））。至此全部 17 个 DQ 已由用户正式决定（Pending Decision Questions = 0）。

### 9.2 推荐架构（Proposed Architecture — 非 Accepted）

> 本节为**推荐架构提案**，供用户审查；**不是**已接受决定、**不是**实施授权。所有组件名均为契约性占位，非生产代码承诺。

#### 9.2.1 端到端 Command 流程（Proposed）

```text
Entrypoint (API/CLI)
   │  仅协议转换，不触 Repository / Domain
   ▼
Application Use Case                        ◄── 拥有业务事务
   │  Prepare：装载 Current Truth（只读短事务）
   │  Execute：经 ModelRuntimePort/RetrievalPort 调用 Skill（无 DB 事务持有）
   │  Commit：BusinessCommitService 单事务原子提交
   ▼
BusinessCommitService（统一事务模板，DEC-035 六要素）
   │  Create Domain Version + Formal Evidence Links
   │  + Update Current Truth Pointer + Update Stage State
   │  + Write Audit + Write Idempotency Record   ──► 同一事务，任一失败整体回滚
   ▼
Durable Work Intent（与业务写入同事务落库）   ◄── API 返回 accepted 前必须可靠记录
   ▼
WorkflowDispatchPort ──（relay/backend 移交 RFC-003）──► Workflow Worker
```

**Unit of Work 边界（DQ-06 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 每个 Transactional Application Command 创建一个新的**一次性 UnitOfWork**（NEW → ACTIVE → COMMITTED / ROLLED_BACK → CLOSED）——一个 UoW 对应一个 SQLAlchemy Session、一个短数据库事务、一个显式业务状态迁移、一个最终 commit 或 rollback 结果；Use Case 显式调用 `commit()`，正常 Context 退出不得自动提交；未成功 commit 退出或异常退出 = rollback → close → discard。**Execute Outside Transaction 阶段不持有任何 UnitOfWork**；Prepare 与 Commit 作为不同 Transactional Command 时使用不同 UoW 实例。

**并发控制边界（DQ-07 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 分层并发控制覆盖上图全流程——Commit 阶段的 BusinessCommitService 使用 DQ-04 `expected_revision` compare-and-swap（零影响行 = 冲突整体回滚），并以命名数据库唯一约束作为重复业务事实的最终防线；**涉及 Worker 执行所有权的流程须先经短事务获取 Durable Lease（获取成功即提交、随即释放行锁/Session/UoW/连接），再于事务外执行 LLM/外部调用，Worker Commit 的最终短事务同时验证 expected_revision + 当前 Lease Holder + 当前 fencing_token + Attempt/Run identity**；队列式领取（如 Durable Work Intent 的 Worker Claim）仅可用 SELECT FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED 短事务（select → lock → assign holder/Lease/fencing token → commit → release → execute outside）。**进程内锁仅减少重复工作、不提供业务正确性；40001/40P01 由 Application Transaction Runner 以全新 UoW/Session 最多三次总尝试重试，语义冲突不盲目重试；外部 Provider 调用不进入事务重试循环。**

**幂等边界（DQ-08 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 上图 Commit 阶段的 `Write Idempotency Record` 遵循分层幂等模型——Business Command Idempotency Record 由执行业务状态修改的模块拥有，其成功状态与 DEC-035 六要素在**同一个 Atomic Business Commit** 中提交（业务回滚不得留下 SUCCEEDED 记录）；Commit 成功但响应丢失时，相同 Idempotency Scope + Key + Input Fingerprint 的后续请求重放原 Application Result（不重复执行业务副作用），不同 Fingerprint 返回 Idempotency Key Conflict。Retry 复用同一 Command ID / Idempotency Key / Stage Run ID / Input Fingerprint 并创建新 Attempt ID；Intentional Rerun 创建新逻辑身份并保留 `rerun_of` 关系。**IN_PROGRESS 执行所有权由 DQ-07 Durable Lease Holder + fencing_token + Attempt ID 保护**（旧 Worker 不得把记录转换为 SUCCEEDED）；Checkpoint/thread_id 不是业务幂等记录。消费端 Dedup Marker 与消费产生的业务更新同事务提交；数据库事务重试不得重新调用已完成的外部 Provider 调用（复用同一 Provider Call Identity）。幂等物理表/状态机/唯一约束均未创建——Idempotency Identity Matrix 为实现前置条件（REQUIRED / NOT AUTHORIZED）。

**Durable Dispatch 边界（DQ-09 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 上图 `Durable Work Intent（与业务写入同事务落库）` 已由 DQ-09 正式决定为 **PostgreSQL-backed Transactional Durable Work Intent**（MVP 内部可靠工作调度模型）——Durable Work Intent 表示「系统必须执行的工作」，**不得与 Integration Event / Audit Record / Workflow Checkpoint / Business Current Truth 混用**；其创建与触发它的业务状态变化处于**同一个 PostgreSQL Atomic Business Commit**（Intent 写入失败整体回滚、回滚不留可领取 Intent），**API 只有在 Intent 成功持久化并 Commit 后才返回 accepted（accepted 仅表示工作已可靠记录，不表示 Worker 或业务执行已完成）**。Worker 领取使用短事务 SELECT FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED Claim（分配 Lease Holder + 单调 fencing_token + Attempt Identity 后提交并释放行锁/Session/UoW/连接），**长执行发生在数据库事务之外**；最终提交在新短事务中重新验证 `dispatch_id` / Lease Holder / fencing_token / Attempt Identity / Input Fingerprint / `expected_revision`（旧 Worker 或过期 Lease 不得完成 Intent）。Retry 保持相同 `dispatch_id` + 新 Delivery Attempt；Intentional Rerun 创建新 `dispatch_id` 并保留 `rerun_of`。**Delivery = at-least-once（不承诺 exactly-once；唯一业务效果由 DQ-08 幂等 + Consumer Dedup + 命名唯一约束 + DQ-07 Lease/Fencing + revision + Atomic Commit 组合保证）**；Database Transaction Retry 与 Work Execution Retry 分离（DQ-07 三次事务尝试不直接套用长任务次数）；LISTEN/NOTIFY 仅为非权威唤醒优化，周期性 Polling 是权威恢复路径。Work Intent 表/状态机/Relay/Worker/Broker/Migration 均未创建；现有 Idempotency Identity Matrix 与 Concurrency Scenario Matrix 须补充 Dispatch 场景——REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED。

**事件与审计边界（DQ-10 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 上图 `Write Audit` 已由 DQ-10 正式决定为 **append-only 权威问责证据**——Audit Record 与对应 Business Current Truth 修改处于**同一个 DEC-035 Atomic Business Commit**（写入失败整体回滚、回滚不留成功 Audit、不得先提交业务再异步补写；更正仅追加 Correction / Superseding / Reversal Record 且引用被更正记录，不得 UPDATE 覆盖或删除）。**六类记录语义独立**：Domain Event（模块内部过去式事实，默认不自动持久化；需同事务执行的 Handler 在最外层 UoW 内 Commit 前执行，不嵌套 UoW、不独立 Commit、不调用外部 Provider）/ Audit Record / State Transition Record（显式类型 Audit Record，可物理共用 Audit Ledger，不代表语义合并，不充当 Integration Event 或 Current Truth）/ Application Event（Commit 后本地 LOCAL / BEST-EFFORT / NON-DURABLE 通知，不承担必须执行工作，Handler 失败不回滚已提交业务）/ Integration Event / Observability Event（RFC-007 非权威 Telemetry，失败不回滚业务、不替代 Audit）。需要可靠跨边界传播的事实在**同一 PostgreSQL Atomic Business Commit** 中写入**独立 Transactional Integration Event Outbox**（与 DQ-09 Durable Work Intent 不共用 Identity / 状态机 / Payload / Retry / Retention），**Delivery = at-least-once（不承诺 exactly-once；Exactly-once Business Effect 由 DQ-08 幂等 + Consumer Dedup + Event Identity + Consumer Scope + 命名唯一约束 + Atomic Commit 组合保证）**；Consumer 依据 Event Identity + Consumer Scope 去重（Dedup Marker 与消费业务更新同事务）。Event Translation / Audit Creation / Integration Event Selection 由 Application 层显式拥有（Repository / ORM Hook / SQLAlchemy Event Listener / 数据库 Trigger 不得隐式决定）。CloudEvents-compatible Envelope 仅可选互操作方向（不替代 Outbox / Atomic Commit / Relay / Retry / Consumer Dedup / Delivery Guarantee）。Audit Ledger / State Transition Record / Outbox 不构成 Business Current Truth，**不引入 Event Sourcing**。Event & Record Classification Table 为持久化实现前置条件——REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED；Audit 表 / State Transition Ledger / Event Outbox / Publisher / Relay / Consumer / Event Registry / Migration 均未创建。

**快照与历史边界（DQ-11 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 上图 `Create Domain Version + Update Current Truth Pointer` 已由 DQ-11 正式决定——**Formal Model = Authoritative Current Truth + Immutable Business Version Snapshots + Append-only Audit/State Transition History + Optional Derived Query Projections**。新 `Create Domain Version` 是**逻辑完整、不可变、独立可读的正式 Business Version Snapshot**（不依赖 Event/Delta 链重放、不经后来变化的 Current Truth Pointer / `current_source_version` / `current_dependency` 等可变引用解释历史；读取历史版本返回当时正式提交语义）；**只有成功完成的正式 Atomic Business Commit 才产生正式 Domain Version**（上图 BusinessCommitService 单事务提交点）——Database/40001/40P01 Retry、Work Execution/Provider Retry、Worker Crash Recovery、Lease Renewal、Heartbeat、Checkpoint Save、Workflow Resume 本身、Telemetry Export、最终回滚事务、未提交 LLM 临时输出均不得产生新正式版本（Intentional Rerun 成功后可产生新版本）。`Update Current Truth Pointer` 使用带独立非空 `revision` 的**显式 Current Truth Pointer** 经 DQ-04 Compare-and-Swap 条件更新（CAS 零行 = 冲突并回滚完整 Atomic Business Commit；Version Number 分配与 Version Insert 同事务、Version Insert 与 Pointer CAS 同事务、冲突/失败事务不留孤立 Domain Version / 已更新 Pointer / 其他部分写入）；**`MAX(version_number)` / `ORDER BY version_number DESC LIMIT 1` 不得用作 Current Truth Selector**，`latest_created_version` / `latest_approved_version` / `current_effective_version` 三者独立语义。Current Version 被 Invalidate 后不删除版本、不静默回退（显式选择 Replacement / Promote Candidate / Restore / `NO_CURRENT_TRUTH`）；**Restore 是新的前向 Business Command**（创建新 `domain_version_id` / `version_number` / `command_id` / audit record 并记录 `restored_from_version_id`，**不是 Database Transaction Rollback**——不同术语、不同 Port、不同 Application Contract、不同测试）。**Query Projection 是派生非权威读取模型**（可从 Current Truth Pointer 与 Version Tables 构建、不要求 Event Stream 重放；Projection 延迟/损坏/丢失/重建不得改变 Business Current Truth；State-changing Command 不得只依赖可能过期 Projection 决定最终 Commit）。**不采用完整 Event Sourcing、不采用 Current-state-only Overwrite、不采用 Delta-only 权威历史、不在 MVP 引入通用 Bitemporal Model / SQL `AS OF` 查询层**；五种 Snapshot 术语（Business Version Snapshot / Workflow Checkpoint / Database Backup Snapshot / Event-Sourcing Snapshot / Query Projection）语义独立。**Aggregate / Invariant Matrix 扩展（Version Boundary / Snapshot Granularity / Current Truth Selector / Promotion / Invalidation / Restore / Historical Dependency 等列）为实现前置条件——REQUIRED 但 NOT AUTHORIZED**；Version Tables / Current Truth Pointer / Projection / Restore / Promotion / Invalidation 实现 / Snapshot Schema / Migration 均未创建。

**来源与证据边界（DQ-12 Accepted Decision，2026-08-02）：** 上图 `Create Domain Version + Formal Evidence Links` 中的证据侧已由 DQ-12 正式决定——**Formal Model = PostgreSQL Authoritative Source/Evidence Graph + Immutable Content-addressed Source Blobs + Versioned Derived Artifacts and Fragments + Explicit Evidence-to-Claim Links + Rebuildable Non-authoritative Retrieval Index**。**PostgreSQL 是 Source/Evidence
